2026年 10月 30日 全5頁

10月会合で日銀は政策金利を据え置き 
2％の物価安定に向け利上げ継続へ 
利上げの悪影響は今のところ限定的、12月に1.5％へ追加利上げか

経済調査部 チーフエコノミスト 神田 慶司 
  エコノミスト 秋元 虹輝

[要約] 
◼ 日本銀行（日銀）は2026年10月29、30日の金融政策決定会合で、短期金利を1.25％
程度に据え置いた。日銀の政策運営は、基調的な物価上昇率を「2％に近づける」局面
から「2％程度で安定させる」局面に移った。今後は、経済・物価・金融情勢に応じて
政策金利を引き上げ、金融緩和の度合いを調整していく方針だ。 
◼ 日銀は2024年 3月以降、金融政策の正常化を進めてきたが、企業・家計への悪影響は
これまでのところ限定的だ。企業では比較的良好な資金調達環境やネット有利子負債
比率の低下が、家計では収入の増加や借入返済額の減少に加え、住宅ローン金利上昇
の返済額への反映の遅れが、足元の負担増を抑えている。ただし、家計の借入残高は
収入対比で高水準にあり、金利高が長期化すれば、返済負担が徐々に高まる可能性が
ある。 
◼ 当社では、日銀は 2026年 12月会合で短期金利を1.5％、2027年 3月会合で 1.75％へ
引き上げた後、いったん据え置くとみている。ただし、その後も金融環境がなお緩和
的で物価上振れリスクが残れば、同年6月会合で2.0％への追加利上げに踏み切る可能
性がある。

2026年 10月会合で日銀は政策金利の据え置きを決定 
日本銀行（日銀）は2026年10月29、30日に開催された金融政策決定会合で、無担保コール
レート・オーバーナイト物（以下、短期金利）を 1.25％程度に据え置くことを決めた1。今回
は利上げを決定した翌月の会合ということもあり、これまでの利上げの影響を見極めることを
重視したとみられる。

1 日本銀行「当面の金融政策運営について」（2026年10月 30日）

### Page 2

1

2 / 5

連続利上げに慎重な姿勢は、前回会合後の総裁発言からもうかがえる。9月会合直後の記者
会見で植田和男総裁は、「大きな幅の利上げとか何回も連続して利上げするというようなこと
は、端的に申し上げれば、物価が目標を超えてものすごい上がっていくというリスクが高いか、
あるいは既に目標を超えちゃっているかというようなことに依存するんだと思います」2と述べ、
その例として2022、23年の米欧の高インフレ局面を挙げた。 
また、日銀が保有する上場投資信託（ETF）については、2026年 1月以降の売却が市場に大
きな影響を与えることなく円滑に進捗したことなどを踏まえ、2027年 1月から年間の売却ペー
スを簿価で 3,300億円程度から 8,600億円程度（市場全体の売買代金に占める年間売却額（時
価）の割合は0.05％程度から0.1％程度）に引き上げることを決めた3。

インフレ基調は2％目標と概ね整合的で、今後は定着に向けて利上げ継続へ 
9月会合の声明文で「2％に近づいている」4と評価した消費者物価の基調的な上昇率につい
ては、10月会合で公表された「経済・物価情勢の展望」（展望レポート）で「2％の『物価安定
の目標』と概ね整合的な水準」に修正された。さらに、「従来は、基調的な物価上昇率を 2％に
向けて引き上げていく過程にあり、物価の下振れリスクに相応の注意を払うことが必要であっ
たが、足もとでは局面が変化しており、今後は、基調的な物価上昇率が 2％を超えて上振れて
いくリスクが顕在化し、それがその後の経済に悪影響を及ぼすことがないよう、十分に留意す
る必要がある」と指摘した5。 
植田総裁は 9月会合直後の記者会見ですでに、基調的な物価上昇率を 2％程度で安定させて
いくことが重要になったとして、 「政策の局面が変化した」と説明していた。10月会合での文
言変更は、こうした政策局面の変化をより明確にしたものといえる。また、10月 16日の記者
会見でも、今後は基調的な物価上昇率が「これ以上上がるということは容認できない」6と述べ、
2％近辺での定着を目指す考えを改めて示した。 
展望レポートでは、引き続き物価上振れリスクへの警戒を示し、経済・物価・金融情勢に応
じて政策金利を引き上げ、金融緩和の度合いを調整していく方針を前回と同様に明記した。日
銀が物価上振れリスクを見極める上で注視しているのが、①中東情勢に伴う原油価格、②AI関
連需要、③為替相場の3つである。足元ではAI関連投資の収益性への懸念が広がり、円安も一
服しているものの、日銀はこれらの動向が国内物価にどの程度波及するかを引き続き見極めて
いくとみられる。 
展望レポートを見ると、2026年度の実質 GDP成長率は春先以降のエネルギー・原材料価格の
上昇を受けて伸び率を縮小しつつも緩やかな成長を続け、2027年度以降は所得から支出への前

2 日本銀行「総裁記者会見」（2026年9月18日、24日公表） 
3 日本銀行「ETFの売却ペースの見直しに関する決定（2026年 10月）」（2026年 10月30日） 
4 日本銀行「金融市場調節方針の変更について」（2026年9月 18日） 
5 日本銀行「経済・物価情勢の展望（2026年10月）」（基本的見解、2026年10月 30日） 
6 日本銀行「植田総裁記者会見(10月 16日) ―G20終了後の植田総裁、三村財務官 共同記者会見における総裁
発言」（2026年10月 20日）

### Page 3

1

3 / 5

向きな循環メカニズムが強まるもとで成長率を緩やかに高めていくとされた（政策委員見通し
の中央値は2026年度＋0.7％、27年度＋0.7％、28年度＋0.8％） 。 
一方、消費者物価（除く生鮮食品）の前年比は、原油価格の上昇や半導体価格等の上昇、為
替円安などを背景に見通し期間前半に 2％をはっきりと上回る水準まで伸び率を高めた後、後
半には原油高の影響の減衰により、 2％程度に向けてプラス幅を縮小する見通しだ（政策委員
見通しの中央値は 2026年度＋2.5％、2027年度＋2.6％、2028年度＋2.0％） 。夏場以降の原油
価格上昇の影響などから、2027年度が前回（7月）から 0.2％pt上方修正された。消費者物価
（除く生鮮食品・エネルギー）の見通しは 2026年度＋2.4％、2027年度＋2.7％、2028年度＋
2.3％と、前回からの修正は小幅にとどまった。なお、2027年 4月から実施される可能性のあ
る飲食料品の消費税率引き下げは、物価見通しに反映されていない。 
利上げが続く中でも企業・家計への影響はこれまでのところ限定的 
日銀は2024年3月の金融政策決定会合でマイナス金利政策の解除とイールドカーブ・コント
ロール（長短金利操作）の撤廃を決め、その後 5回の利上げや国債買入れの減額を実施するな
ど金融政策の正常化を進めてきた。 
もっとも、企業や家計の経済活動への悪影響はこれまでのところ限定的で、金融環境はなお
緩和的とみられる。まず企業部門を見ると、日銀短観の業況判断DIは高水準を維持しており、
資金繰り判断DIや金融機関の貸出態度判断DIにも目立った悪化は見られない（図表1左）。中
小企業でも顕著な悪化は見られず、利上げが進む中でも、企業の景況感や資金調達環境は総じ
て良好な状態にある。 
ただし、過去の利上げ局面との単純な比較には注意が必要だ。1990年頃までの利上げは、資
産バブル期を含む高成長・高インフレ環境の下で実施されたのに対し、 2000年代以降は低成
長・デフレないし低インフレが長く続き、企業の財務構造も大きく変化した。足元で利上げの
影響が表れにくい背景には、企業の財務体質の改善がある。 
有利子負債から現預金を控除した額の総資産に対する比率（ネット有利子負債比率）は、資
本金 1千万円以上 10億円未満の中小・中堅企業で歴史的な低水準にある（図表 1右）。大企業
では中小・中堅企業ほど低下しておらず、資本金 1千万円未満の企業では相対的に高いものの、
企業部門全体では 1970～90 年代に比べて大幅に低下している。こうした財務構造の変化によ
り、政策金利が上昇しても、企業部門全体では利払い費の増加を通じた金融引き締めの影響が
以前より表れにくくなっていると考えられる。

図表 1：日銀短観の各種 DIの長期推移（左） 、企業規模別に見たネット有利子負債比率（右）

（注）左図は全産業ベースで、破線で示した中小企業は資本金 2千万円以上 1億円未満。 「利上げ局面」は利上
げ開始から利下げ直前までの期間。右図のネット有利子負債比率は、有利子負債から現預金を控除して総資産
で除したもの。資本金 1千万円未満のデータは年度値の平均（その他は四半期値の平均） 。 
（出所）日本銀行、財務省より大和総研作成

家計部門についても、これまでのところ金利上昇の影響は限定的だ。ただし、ストック面か
ら金利上昇への耐性を見ると、借入残高対年収比率は 2000年代初めから大幅に上昇している
（図表 2左）。データが遡及可能な2002年と比べると、2026年上半期の同比率は約53％pt高い。
要因別では、借入残高の増加が同比率を約 66％pt押し上げた一方、年収の増加が約 12％pt押
し下げた。家計の借入負担はストック面で以前より重くなっており、金利上昇が長期化すれば、
その影響が徐々に顕在化する可能性がある。 
図表 2：家計の借入残高対年収比率（左） 、借入返済負担率（右）の2002年からの変化幅

（注）二人以上の勤労者世帯。左図の借入残高対年収比率は、1 世帯あたりの借入残高（「住宅・土地のための
負債」と「住宅・土地以外の負債」の合計）を年間世帯収入で除したもの。右図の借入返済負担率は 1世帯あ
たりの実収入に対する借金返済額の比率。「低所得世帯」は年間収入五分位階級のうち第Ⅰ・Ⅱ分位、「高所得
世帯」は第Ⅳ・Ⅴ分位を集計。「返済世帯比率要因」は、住宅ローン返済世帯の収入が二人以上の勤労者世帯
全体の収入に占める割合の変化による寄与を表す。 
（出所）総務省より大和総研作成

### Page 5

1

5 / 5

一方、フロー面から足元の資金繰りを見ると、収入に対する借入返済額の比率（借入返済負
担率）は、返済額の減少や収入の増加を背景に、2002年以降の低水準にある（図表2右）。2026
年上半期の借入返済負担率は2002年を約1.1％pt下回った。要因別では、住宅ローン返済世帯
の収入シェア（住宅ローン返済世帯の収入が二人以上の勤労者世帯全体の収入に占める割合）
の上昇が負担率を押し上げた一方、返済額の減少と収入の増加が押し下げた。所得階級別に見
ると、足元では低所得世帯（年収下位 4割）の返済負担率が相対的に上昇しているものの、低
所得世帯、高所得世帯（年収上位4割）ともに2002年の水準を下回る。このため、金利上昇が
家計の資金繰りを大きく圧迫する状況には至っていない。 
ただし、足元の返済負担の低さは今後も続くとは限らない。変動金利型住宅ローンでは、毎
月の返済額の急増を抑える「5 年ルール」や「125％ルール」が広く採用されている7。こうし
た仕組みは、金利上昇の影響が返済額に反映される時期を遅らせ、短期的な負担増を和らげる
半面、負担の顕在化を将来に先送りする。借入残高が高水準にあることも踏まえると、金利高
が長期化するほど家計の返済負担が高まる可能性には留意が必要だ。

中立金利を探りながら、日銀は段階的な利上げを継続へ 
当社では、日銀は次回 2026年 12月の金融政策決定会合で短期金利を1.5％に、2027年 3月
会合で 1.75％に引き上げるとみている。その後は経済・物価・金融情勢への影響を見極めるた
め、いったん政策金利を据え置くと予想する。ただし、1.75％への引き上げ後も金融環境がな
お緩和的で、物価上振れリスクが残る場合には、同年 6月会合で 2.0％への追加利上げに踏み
切る可能性がある。 
日銀は2026年3月に公表したレポート8で、6つのモデルによる自然利子率の推計レンジを▲
0.9％程度～＋0.5％程度と示した。2％の物価上昇率を想定すると、名目の中立金利は 1.1～
2.5％程度で、1.75％はそのレンジのほぼ中央に位置する。だが、中立金利を正確に推計する
ことは困難で、その推計レンジには大きな幅がある9。そのため日銀は、各種データでこれまで
の利上げの影響や物価の基調などを確認しながら、金融緩和の度合いを徐々に調整していくと
みられる。

7 5年ルールは、変動金利型住宅ローンの金利が変動しても、毎月の返済額を5年間据え置く仕組み。125％ル
ールは、返済額の見直し時に、新たな返済額の上限を従来の 125％とする仕組み。いずれも法令で一律に定め
られたものではなく、適用の有無は金融機関や商品によって異なる。 
8 日本銀行「自然利子率の動向と金融緩和度合いの評価」（「日銀レビュー」、2026-J-5、2026年3月） 
9 2026年6月会合後の記者会見で内田眞一副総裁は、中立金利の推計値には「相当なばらつき」があるとした
上で、「短期金利を上げていく過程で、それを含めた金融環境の変化ということを点検して、中立金利の水準
を探りながら、金融緩和の度合いを調整していく」と述べた（日本銀行「総裁記者会見（内田副総裁代理）」
（2026年6月16日、17日公表））。

市場焦點
聯準會9月
升息一碼
• 聯邦基準利率升至 3.75%~4.00%，經濟預測部分上調2026-2028年GDP成長率、失業率皆下
調，通膨降溫進程仍相對緩慢 ，點陣圖顯示今年還有升息一碼的機會 ，這次升息對股票而言 ，
聯準會沒有準備以大幅犧牲經濟與就業換取通膨下降 ，降低了硬著陸風險；對債券而言 ，短
端仍須消化更高的政策利率，但長端反而可能受惠於抗通膨可信度改善。
AI模型
開發放緩
• Anthropic、OpenAI及xAI相繼支持放緩最先進AI模型開發速度。不過，前沿模型開發速度放
緩，並不代表AI基礎建設需求同步轉弱。晶片、算力、資料中心及能源等AI基礎建設仍處於供
不應求格局 ，短期若因市場情緒或資金槓桿調整而出現回檔 ，較可能反映估值與持倉修正 ，

而非AI需求基本面反轉 。後續若前期累積的槓桿壓力逐步消化 ，且全球總體經濟及地緣政治
風險未進一步惡化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值。
台灣央行
• 利率決議重點： (1)台灣央行宣布基準利率維持不變 ，重貼現率、擔保放款融通利率和短期融
通利率分別維持2%、2.375%和4.25%。(2)放鬆房市信用管制 ，調整自然人第2戶購屋貸款最
高成數上限，由6成調升為7成。(3)上修今年經濟成長率及通膨率 ，GDP上修至11.48%、CPI
及核心CPI分別上修至2.03%及2.16。(4)央行預估明年AI基礎建設需求將持續貢獻台灣出口表
現，不過由於基期已墊高，預測明年台灣經濟成長率為5.82%。

資料來源：Fed，華爾街見聞，2026.9.17
貨幣決議與9月、7月聲明稿差異，不再強調能源衝擊 全員支持升息
• 聯準會9月升息一碼，聯邦基準利率在3.75%~4.00%。
聲明稿重點差異
紅色字體為9月的新增部分，括號內藍色字體為刪除的7月聲明措辭
FOMC以12票贊成升息一碼 批准發布以下聲明 ：委員會決定 ，將聯邦基金利率目標 區間上調至 3.75%至4%維持在
3.50%至3.75%，以支持聯準會的雙重使命。委員會延續其維持銀行體系充足準備金的政策。
儘管中東衝突在一定程度上帶來了高水準的不確定性 ，美國經濟活動持續以穩健步調擴張 。儘管地緣政治情勢 等因素
使經濟前景仍籠 罩高度不確定性，但國內支出保持韌性，生產力成長強勁，企業資本投資也相當強勁穩健。新增就業

人數大致跟上勞動力成長速度，失業率也變化不大，顯示就業市場目前仍大致維持穩定。
相較於委員會2%的目標，通貨膨脹率依然偏高 ，這在一定程度上反映了部分特定部門 (包括能源)因供給衝擊所導致的
價格上漲。今日的政策行動，將有助於讓通膨更及時地回到2%目標，委員會將實現價格穩定。

聯準會會後記者會問答重點整理
1
通膨「過高且持續太久」 當
前重心偏向物價穩定
通膨數據未改善，通膨過高且持續太久，聯準會當前的政策重心偏向雙重使命中的物價穩定 。透過政策
行動表明，聯準會對抑制通膨是認真的。
2
經濟、就業與地緣政治促成
一致升息
近幾週公布的數據證實美國經濟正在走強 ，基礎經濟成長率高於先前判斷；其次 ，勞動市場依然穩健；
第三，通膨持續高於目標，地緣政治局勢也改變經濟展望 。華許表示，這三項因素共同促成當天「堅定
且一致」的決定。
3
點狀圖暗示今年再升 1碼 但
不是政策承諾
最新經濟預測顯示，多數官員認為今年仍有進一步升息空間。12 名官員預期年底前再升息 1 碼，另有 4
人預期還會升息 2 碼，僅 2 人主張利率維持在目前水準。不過，點狀圖反映的是官員對適當利率路徑的

個別預測，並非正式的承諾。
4
拒絕提供前瞻指引 不預判下
次會議結果
面對下一次升息可能落在 10 月或 12 月的追問，華許表示「我不給出前瞻指引。」
他強調，先前公開表達的是對貨幣政策紀律的承諾 ，而不是對特定利率決策的承諾 。聯準會不會預判未
來任何會議結果，後續行動仍將依據最新經濟數據及展望作出決定。
5
升息非市場逼迫 迴避川普施
壓問題
面對此次升息是否由債市波動及市場壓力所推動的提問，華許予以否認。
他表示是根據就業走勢、經濟強度及整體情勢作出決定 ，市場價格可作為觀察依據，但不能決定聯準會
的政策。
資料來源：Fed，華爾街見聞，2026.9.17

• 市場將華許記者會解讀為偏鷹，那斯達克指數、標普
500指數、道瓊工業指數皆下跌。

4.9323%，隨後回升至接近 4.99%，在關鍵的5%關
口附近波動。
• 2年期殖利率則由盤中低點4.5975%升至4.7124%，
反映投資人重新提高對後續升息的預期。
• 黃金跌破4,260美元。
• 利率市場顯示 ，交易員加大 聯準會在2026 年底前進
一步升息的押注，12月升息機率升至約50%。

響，逢拉回就分批買進，相對看好美、台、韓相關供
應鏈
• 債市：預期美債利率 維持高位震盪格局 ，目前債市

配置仍以鎖利為主，且聚焦中短天期。

台灣利率連10凍 明年經濟展望佳
• 利率決議重點： (1)台灣央行宣布基準利率維持不變 ，重貼現率、擔保放款融通利率和短期融通利率分別維持 2%、2.375%
和4.25%。(2)放鬆房市信用管制，調整自然人第2戶購屋貸款最高成數上限，由6成調升為7成。(3)上修今年經濟成長率及通
膨率，GDP上修至11.48%(六月預估9.45 %)、CPI及核心CPI分別上修至2.03%及2.16%(六月預估1.91%及1.90%)。
• 觀點：全球AI資本支出維持上調的趨勢，台股供應鏈訂單直接受惠，央行預估明年AI基礎建設需求將持續貢獻台灣出口表現，
不過由於基期已墊高，預測明年台灣經濟成長率為5.82%。
• 台股短線展望：上週隨著聯準會利率決議公布後，台股量能回升，顯示短線消息面或已利空出盡。權值股台積電領軍台灣加

權指數站上月線，技術指標偏正向。投資佈局方面，由於地緣政治、通膨及貿易關稅風險仍存在不確定性，建議AI概念持股
核心布局行業龍頭企業，留意中小型成長股較易受到外部因素而股價大幅波動。
資料來源：中央銀行，2026.9.17。f代表央行預測數。

美國零售強勁回升擴大內需支撐，惟價格壓力與升息提高市場波動
• 零售全面回升，消費動能展現廣度：美國8月零售銷售月增1.2%，創3月以來最大增幅，優於市場預期，並扭轉7月修正
後月減0.5%；非實體店面銷售月增 2.6%、電子及家電增 1.6%，餐飲服務亦增 1.2%，且扣除汽車與汽油後仍增 1.2%，
控制組更增1.4%，顯示消費回升並非僅由汽車與燃料支出帶動，第三季內需仍具支撐。
• 名目消費偏強，通膨與升息增添需求壓力： 不過8月零售銷售年增6.0%屬名目增幅，部分反映物價上升因素；同期 CPI
年增3.4%，汽油價格月增3.9%，能源成本上升可能壓縮家庭實質購買力 ，加上Fed於9月決議升息1碼，後續利率政策
仍將視通膨與經濟數據調整，使市場焦點由消費回升轉向評估高利率與高物價對後續需求動能的影響。

• 後市觀察：短期零售回升仍有助支撐美國景氣，但若能源成本持續走高，加上升息後借貸成本上升，消費動能能否維持
將成為重要觀察點；投資配置可偏向美國投資級短中天期債券 ，兼顧收益與利率風險，股票則可聚焦獲利穩定、具定價
能力的大型優質企業，降低需求放緩與資金成本升高的影響。
近三月美國零售銷售月率變化(%)
資料來源： U.S. Census Bureau, Advance Monthly Retail Trade Survey ，2026.9.17
零售銷售類別月增率比較

顧 • AI安全監管升溫，短期壓抑市場情緒：Anthropic、OpenAI及xAI相繼支持放緩最先進AI模型開發速度，並引入獨立第三方
評估等安全機制，引發市場對AI資本支出降溫的疑慮。不過，前沿模型開發速度放緩，並不代表AI基礎建設需求同步轉弱。
Alphabet、微軟及亞馬遜近期AI相關積壓訂單持續攀升，合計達約1.64兆美元，其中微軟及Alphabet分別突破6,000億及
5,000億美元，搭配雲端業務持續成長，顯示企業AI需求仍具韌性，相關訂單有望逐步轉化為未來數年的營收與獲利。
• 槓桿壓力逐步消化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值：美國雲端巨頭擴大AI資本支出，壓抑短期自由現金流；相較之下，亞洲
科技企業自由現金流可望由目前約2,450億增至2028年的6,330億美元，支撐亞洲科技供應鏈中長期發展。短期若因市場情

緒或資金槓桿調整而出現回檔，較可能反映估值與持倉修正，而非AI需求基本面反轉。後續若前期累積的槓桿壓力逐步消化，
且全球總體經濟及地緣政治風險未進一步惡化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值。
AI三巨頭齊喊放慢模型研發
美國雲端巨頭與亞太科技企業自由現金流
資料來源：摩根投信，2026.8.31

油價在美國中期選舉前，或將維持在高檔
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.14
伊朗戰爭期間卡達油氣收入暴跌
卡達政府的石油和天然氣收入 美伊衝突
• 美伊衝突短期內仍難見明確解方，彭博預估雙方後續可能採取的行動，主要可分為以下四種情境：
(1)伊朗承受經濟封鎖：伊朗承受美國經濟制裁與施壓，避免進一步重大軍事升級。在美方護航行動支持下，商船仍可維持通行
荷姆茲海峽，國際原油供應未出現明顯中斷，油價預估在每桶80美元區間波動。
(2)伊朗將攻擊範圍限於海灣地區油輪與美軍：伊朗主要鎖定海灣地區能源運輸船隻及美軍目標，美方則採取有限度軍事回應，

衝突維持在一定範圍內，油價預估在每桶90美元區間波動。
(3)伊朗擴大攻擊範圍：攻擊目標不再局限於海灣地區船隻及美軍基地，海灣能源基礎設施以及紅海等重要航運咽喉，均可能成
為目標。局勢升級期間，油價可能維持在每桶100美元以上，待衝突緩和後回落至70至80美元區間，此情境發生的可能性最高。
(4)美伊雙方全面擴大開戰：伊朗進一步擴大攻擊目標，導致美國軍事資產及區域能源基礎設施遭受重大破壞，甚至不排除以色
列重新加入戰局，油價可能升至每桶120美元。

全球債市拋售勢頭不墜，AI融資競逐資金推升美債殖利率
• 長端殖利率走高，財政與通膨壓力仍是主因 ：近期美國長
天期公債殖利率持續走高，其中10年期美債殖利率更突破
5%，創2007年以來高點；除能源價格上升推升通膨疑慮
外，美國財政赤字、政府債務及持續性的發債需求 ，也提
高市場對長天期公債供給與期限溢酬的要求 ，反映長端利
率壓力仍未完全紓解。
• AI企業擴大融資，增加公司債供給與資金競逐： AI資料中
心、晶片與電力基礎建設的資本支出快速增加 ，帶動大型
科技企業提高債券融資規模；截至 6月，華爾街市場策略
研究機構Yardeni Research在追蹤外國私人投資人過去一
年買進美國公司債約3,900億美元，高於美國公債約3,290
億美元，顯示資金配置確有轉向公司債的現象 ，不過市場

研究亦指出，AI企業發債對美債殖利率的直接推升效果相
對有限，應可視為推動長天期利率上行的眾多因素之一。
• 後市觀察：若財政融資與AI基礎建設支出持續維持高檔 ，
長天期公債仍面臨較大的供給與資金配置壓力；但 AI投資
仍具中長期成長動能 ，配置上可關注資料中心 、半導體及
電力基礎建設供應鏈 ，固定收益則以短中天期美債及高品
質投資級公司債為主，降低長天期利率波動的影響。

新興市場本幣債券擁高票息提供防禦力
• 高票息提供防禦力：對債券投資人而言，票息就像安全墊。當市場利率上升、債券價格下跌時，持續收到的票息可以部分甚
至完全抵銷價格損失。新興市場本幣政府債券今年的表現，比市場原先預期更具韌性。目前新興市場本幣債券的持有收益約
為4.54%，已經足以對利率風險形成相當程度的保護 ，即使今年存續期間對多數市場造成負面影響 ，票息收益仍讓整體報酬
維持正數。這也是為什麼新興市場本幣債券在美伊衝突爆發後一度重挫超過 6%，但之後仍能逐步反彈 。哥倫比亞、巴西、
匈牙利及墨西哥的債券表現尤其突出。
• 市場已反映相當程度的升息風險：問題在於未來幾個月投資環境可能比上半年更加困難，能源價格上漲推升通膨，也迫使新
興市場央行維持較為鷹派的貨幣政策 。市場目前甚至預期 ，19個主要新興市場中 ，有18個市場的債券殖利率可能在年底前

上升。殖利率上升意味著債券價格承壓，因此投資人不能只看表面的高票息，必須同時評估未來利率變化可能造成資本損失。

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.15

新興市場本幣債券目前最大風險在於利率與匯率
• 存續期間仍是今年最主要的拖累因素：一般而言，債券的存續期間越高，當殖利率上升時，債券價格下跌的幅度就可能越大。
今年19個符合基準指數條件的新興市場中 ，有14個市場的總報酬受到存續期間拖累 ，其中韓國、馬來西亞、印尼、泰國及
菲律賓受到的負面影響最為明顯。更值得注意的是，新興市場債券的利率表現與美國公債高度相關，因此，投資新興市場債
券，不能只觀察當地央行，也必須密切留意美國聯準會利率政策及美國公債殖利率走勢。
• 匯率可能成為下一個重要變數：新興市場本幣債券除了債券價格本身，還存在另一項重要風險：匯率。今年即期匯率變動反
而對新興市場本幣債券帶來正面貢獻 ，約增加57個基點。但如果能源價格持續上漲並形成滯脹環境 ，順周期貨幣可能面臨

更大的貶值壓力 。目前市場預期年底前新興市場貨幣整體仍偏弱 ，19種新興市場貨幣中 ，有11種預計兌美元貶值 。其中土
耳其、哥倫比亞及巴西貨幣的潛在跌幅相對較大。這代表即使債券本身提供不錯的票息，如果當地貨幣大幅貶值，換算成美
元或其他投資人本國貨幣後，仍可能侵蝕甚至吞噬債券收益。
• 小結與投資建議：目前新興市場本幣債券比較像是高票息換取較高波動的投資，而不是單純追求資本利得的交易。在殖利率
已處於相對高檔、但利率與匯率風險仍未完全解除的情況下，採取分散市場、控制部位、逢回布局的策略會比追高更為合適。

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.15

• 多重資產配置可有效風險管理：美伊衝突持續及聯準會利率政策，使今年金融市場震盪加劇，加上第四季美國期中選舉到來，
增加市場更多不確定性，不過從歷史經驗來看，地緣政治事件對股市的影響多屬短期現象，市場在初期情緒性下跌後，通常會
逐步回歸基本面，相較於預測事件發展，投資人更應透過多元資產配置分散風險，以提升投資組合的穩定性與長期抗震能力。
• 透過多重資產廣納收益來源：在利率維持相對高檔的環境下，投資人更應重視收益來源的多元化，從下圖可見，相較於過去幾
年，掩護性買權、非投資等級債，及投資等級債等資產的收益率均顯著提升，提供投資組合更具吸引力的現金流來源，其中掩
護性買權收益率已超過10%，非投資等級債，及投資等級債亦分別達7%及5%以上水準，透過結合不同收益型資產，不僅有助

提升整體收益潛力，更能降低對單一市場或利率變動的依賴，在市場波動加劇時提高投資組合的穩定度與韌性，兼顧收益與長
期成長目標。

資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。

公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良

8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。

Update
Accton Technology Corporation  | Asia Pacific
Read-across from Celestica's
3Q26 Results
We see positive implications for Accton given demand strength
(1.6T migration amid sustaining 800G) and breadth (scale up
opportunities) of ethernet  switches in future.
Celestica (CLS.N, not covered) 3Q26 results:
• 3Q26 revenue of US$5,830mn (+24% QoQ/+83% YoY), non-GAAP operating
margin of 8.5%,  a record high, and non-GAAP EPS of US$3.30 (+30% QoQ/

+109% YoY).
° Higher than guidance and 7%/13% above consensus for revenue/earnings,
respectively.
° Drivers: Stronger CCS revenue (84% of the mix in 3Q26), including
networking and compute/storage growth of 68% and 242% YoY,
respectively, vs. guidance 60% and 190% YoY.
• 4Q26 revenue guided at US$6,275-6,575mn (+10% QoQ/+76% YoY at the
midpoint) vs. consensus at US$6,396mn, management raising 2026 target to
US$21bn for revenue (+69% YoY vs. prior US$20.5bn) and US$11.55 for non-

GAAP EPS  (+91% YoY vs. prior US$11.3).
° Driven by hyperscaler-led 800G demand and ramping 1.6T programs,
along with continued AI compute and storage demand.
• 2027 revenue guided at US$40bn (+90% YoY), non-GAAP OPM at 9.0%
(+0.6ppt YoY) and non-GAAP EPS at US$21.5 (+86% YoY)
° It expects CCS to grow 110% (1.6T/800G on networking and ASIC ramp
on compute/storage) and  ATS at 10%, with revenue skewed to  2H on
multiple large ramps and momentum likely sustaining into  2028.

• Pluggable should remain the mainstream interconnect solution, while CPO
might pick up more meaningfully from 3.2T, per company.
• CLS expects ethernet scale up could represent new opportunities in future.
Our view:
• While the customer profiles are different, we still see positive implications
from Celestica's comments for Accton regarding demand strength (1.6T
migration amid sustaining 800G) and breadth (scale up opportunities) of
ethernet  switches.

• We stay constructive on Accton, and believe   it will continue to benefit from
the secular growth in network switches amid increasing data traffic, spec
upgrades, and a wider customer base as well as the AI accelerator modules
on the initiatives for compute diversity.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+

### Page 3

Update
Morgan Stanley Research 3
More Takeaways
Further comments from Celestica:
• Demand visibility is lengthening and it is now having conversations with customers
for 2029/2030 projects
• Still seeing lots of pluggables during 1.6T, while it expects CPO to play out more
prominently in 3.2T.
• It has won a new hyperscaler program to co-design and manufacture a 1.6T rack-
scale CPO networking solution, with production scheduled to ramp in late 2027,

per management.

### Page 4

M
Update
4
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Accton Technology Corporation (2345.TW)
Base case, P/E, which we believe better captures the company's earnings growth momentum.
We assign a target P/E of 30x to our 2027 EPS estimate, which we view as justified by an
operating profit CAGR of 48% for 2025-28, based on our estimates. This implies a PEG of
around 0.6, vs. its average of 0.7 over 2019-25, when the stock was trading at a P/E of 22x for
an operating profit CAGR of 32%.

Risks to Upside
n Stronger data center capex
n Faster network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand losing share in AI networks
n Stronger custom AI accelerator growth
n Stronger ODM direct business model adoption
Risks to Downside
n Weaker data center capex
n Slower network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand taking more share in AI networks
n Softer custom AI accelerator growth
n Softer ODM direct business model adoption

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 3

ASE Technology Holding (3711.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
3
Stable traditional businesses and EMS outlook — Traditional packaging and test
demand is improving, supported by utilization gains and a recovery in automotive
and industrial, as well as AI-adjacent chips. Management expects general-market
business to grow again in 2027, despite softer smartphone and PC demand. For
2027, ASEH expects further EMS growth, led by high-performance computing, and

is pursuing system-level solutions that combine EMS capabilities with ATM
technologies.
LEAP upside intact; GM upward trend structural
Following the solid 3Q26 result, it expects 4Q26 consolidated revenue to grow 6–
8% QoQ, with gross margin of 23–24% and operating margin of 14.5–15.5%. ATM
revenue is forecast to increase 11–13% QoQ, with GM improving 100–200 bps. EMS
revenue is expected to be flat QoQ, with an operating margin of about 2%.
Looking into 2027, management expects LEAP revenue to double, with further GM

improvement as LEAP ramps and utilization rises across traditional packaging and
test. Demand remains ahead of available capacity: assembly is expected to grow
faster than test, with investment prioritizing assembly and wafer probing. Full-
process revenue is projected to exceed US$1bn in 2027, while CPO is expected to
contribute more than US$500m. Capacity and facility space remain key
constraints; ASE plans to increase capex to US$11.5bn in 2027 given its raised

2026 outlook. It also expects traditional-market growth to continue, supported by
automotive, industrial and AI-adjacent demand, despite softer smartphone and PC
markets.
On the back of its structural GM upside and more LEAP revenue contribution, we
lift our 2026/2027 /2028 earnings projection by 12%/18%/16%. We maintain Buy
with TP NT$908, based on an unchanged 25x 2027 EPS.

Bull/Bear: ASE Technology Holding (3711.TW)

### Page 8

ASE Technology Holding (3711.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
8
ASE Technology Holding
Company description
Advanced Semiconductor Engineering (ASE), incorporated in 1984 and listed
at the TWSE in 1989, is one of the world's leading companies in the
semiconductor packaging and testing sector. In June 2016, ASE entered into a
Joint Share Exchange Agreement with SPIL under which ASE Industrial was
eventually to be formed as a holding company with 100% equity interests in

both ASE and SPIL. ASE Industrial was listed on Taiwan's main board on April
30, 2018, the same day on which ASE and SPIL were delisted.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on ASE Holding. The combined ASE-SPIL entity is the
highest-quality OSAT worldwide, in our view, with its market share
leadership, superior operations, economies of scale, and customer/product
diversification. Conditions imposed by the Chinese government on the ASE-
SPIL merger, which required them to operate independently, were lifted in

2020. Consequently, we believe ASE Holding will benefit from further revenue
and operating synergies as well as the trend for advanced packaging.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$908 is based on 25x 2027 EPS average. Our PER is
higher than its five-year range as we expect ASEH to see accelerating
earnings growth in the coming years with GM at upward expansion trend,
mainly driven by its promising outlook in LEAP business and strong expansion
for long-term AI demand. At our target price, the shares would trade at a

2026/27E P/B of 10x/8x.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent ASE shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) poor efficiency and less synergy created from merger with
SPIL; 2) failure to gain market share in advanced packaging; 3) industry
oversupply due to aggressive capacity expansion; 4) severe pricing erosion
due to competition; and 5) a longer-than-expected semiconductor inventory
correction.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

buy recommendation and a Catalyst Watch/STV Downside call corresponds to a sell recommendation. Any stock not assigned

Bull/Bear: Yageo (2327.TW)

### Page 8

Yageo (2327.TW)
30 October 2026 Citi Research
8
Yageo
Company description
Established in 1977 and listed in 1993, Yageo is a leading supplier of passive
electronic components based in Taiwan. The company offers a wide array of
products including MLCC, chip resistors, magnetics, tantalum, and other
components, aiming to be the total solution provider for its customers. By
capacity, the company currently ranks No. 1 in chip resistor and tantalum and

No. 3 in MLCC and inductor globally. Key customers of Yageo include leading
EMS, ODMs, and distributors. The company also has 29 sales offices, 51
manufacturing sites, and 20 R&D centers in 29 nations worldwide.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Yageo shares. Yageo is widely known as a passive
electronic component supplier. Although passive components are generally
viewed as a commodity, Yageo is no longer a commodity play, in our view,

given the company’s foray into premium markets through M&As, which
should help mitigate the impact of sector cyclicality and order volatility. We
expect a more diversified product portfolio to drive stable earnings and better
margins, while improving utilization in its commodity component business
following the end of inventory digestions should add additional fuel.
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,000, based on 23x average 2027E-28E
earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the company’s earnings

performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market.
Our target multiple of 23x is at the higher end of the trading range since 2022
but in line with global peers average. During this period the company has gone
through the cycles, successfully integrated the acquired business to enhance
its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be

### Page 1

기아 (000270)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.15  
잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기

3Q26 Preview 
기아는 3Q26E 매출액 31.1조원(+8.2% YoY, -6.0% QoQ), 영업이익 2.2조원
(+53.1% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 7.2%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 
물량은 좋았으나, 아쉬운 믹스와 환율 
글로벌 도매 판매 84.7만대(+7.8% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 83.0만대
(+8.6%)를 기록했다. EV·HEV 확대에 따른 ASP 상승은 지속되고 있다. 전쟁으로 인
한 원자재 상승으로 3분기 재료비 부담은 전분기 대비 3,000억~4,000억원 증가할 
것으로 예상된다. EV 판매 비중은 2분기 13%에서 3분기 15%, 유럽은 35%에서 
43%로 확대되며 유럽 내 마진 희석은 불가피하다. 원화 강세에 따른 영업상 부담은 
판매보증충당부채 환입 등으로 상쇄돼 3분기 환율 영향은 제한적일 전망이다. 판매보
증충당부채 환입은 QoQ +5,000억원 수준으로 추정된다. 4분기부터 환율 효과는 부정
적일 것이며, 환율 10원 변동 시 연간 손익 민감도는 약 1,500억원 수준이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 165,000원으로 하향 
미국 조지아 공장의 텔루라이드 HEV(2.8만대)와 HMGMA의 스포티지 HEV(1.0만대) 
신규 생산으로 북미의 견조한 HEV 수요에 대응하며, 2027년 믹스 개선이 기대된다. 
유럽은 EV3·EV5로 점유율을 확대하는 가운데 유럽 내 할인율 하락과 EV 잔존가치 
회복으로 판매 여건이 개선되고 있다. 향후 IAA 규제에 따른 법인·플릿 혜택 제한은 
중국 업체의 원가 우위를 줄일 수 있을 전망이다. 
원화 약세 지속을 반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다.  
다만, 현재 주가는 12M Fwd P/E 기준 4.8배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 
수준, 저가 매수 기회라 판단된다. 현재 배당 수익률은 6% 수준으로, 상대적으로 높은 
배당 매력은 주가 하방을 지지할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr

TUẦN 12-16/10 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh 
khoản suy giảm

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
VN-Index có thể hồi kỹ thuật từ cạnh dưới tam giác, song việc chớm thủng hỗ trợ 
1.710 điểm và số mã còn giữ xu hướng tăng từ đáy tháng 7 suy gi ảm khiến xu 
hướng ngắn hạn trở nên trung tính.

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dầu khí, Bảo hiểm và Dịch vụ phân phối là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -3,013 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã MCH, VIC và FPT; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 
887 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -2,021 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -4,384 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng -
4,275 hợp đồng và đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -7,815 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ tư liên tiếp, khi VN-Index lùi về đóng cửa 1.706 
điểm - giảm khoảng 1,7% so với tuần trước và đang dao động quanh vùng hỗ trợ tâm lý 
1.700 điểm, cũng là vùng đáy cũ của tháng 8. Thanh khoản thị trường tiếp tục suy yếu, 
phản ánh tâm lý thận trọng cao độ trong bối cảnh thị trường thiếu vắng thông tin hỗ trợ 
trong nước, trong khi các yếu tố bên ngoài như xung đột tại Iran, và chi phí vốn toàn cầu 
chưa hạ nhiệt tiếp tục đè nặng tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Khối ngoại duy trì chuỗi bán ròng kéo 
dài nhiều phiên liên tiếp, nối dài đà rút vốn đã lên tới xấp xỉ 95 nghìn tỷ đồng từ đầu năm. 
Trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo kết quả kinh doanh quý 3 được kỳ vọng là chất xúc 
tác giúp thị trường giao dịch cân bằng trở lại.

Chỉ số VNINDEX

Source: Trading View, KB Securities Vietnam

Ngắn hạn 
(Dưới 3 tháng)

Trung hạn (Từ 3 đến 9 tháng)

Chỉ báo  Nhận định xu hướng Hành động 
Xanh đậm  Khả năng tăng điểm cao Mua mạnh 
Xanh nhạt  Khả năng tăng điểm vừa phải Mua từng phần 
Vàng  Xu hướng trung tính Mua/Bán trading

Đỏ  Khả năng giảm điểm cao Bán mạnh

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tiếp tục hình thành mẫu nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên đồ thị tuần, đánh dấu tuần 
giảm thứ 4 liên tiếp, cho thấy áp lực phân phối vẫn đang có phần lấn át. Trên khung ngày, 
tuy chỉ số đã có nỗ lực phục hồi trở lại trong 3 phiên đan xen, đặc biệt là phản ứng trong 
phiên cuối tuần khi chạm trendline trung hạn, nhưng việc chỉ có thể hình thành các mẫu 
nến "spinning" hàm ý đà tích lũy chưa thực sự thuyết phục. Trong một vài phiên kế tiếp, 
VNINDEX có thể xuất hiện nhịp hồi kỹ thuật từ vùng hỗ trợ, tương ứng với cạnh dưới tam 
giác, nhưng sau khi chớm phá vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1710 và hiện chỉ còn một số ít các mã giữ 
được xu hướng tăng tính từ đáy tháng 7, xu hướng ngắn hạn đang trở nên khá trung tính.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1780 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1740 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1680 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1630 (+-15)

### Page 4

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu 
Mã Giá đóng cửa tuần Biến động từ đầu tháng Giá khuyến nghị mua Giá mục tiêu Lợi nhuận  kỳ vọng Điểm nhấn đầu tư Báo cáo phân tích

Năm 2026 dự kiến mở thêm 400-450 cửa hàng (+20%YoY) với DT trên mỗi cửa hàng cũ tăng 
trưởng 6%-8%/năm

chiếm 50% thị phần doanh thu ngành bán lẻ dược phẩm, từ mức 17%-20% hiện tại

2 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Consumers Feel One Way, but Spend Another
Kirsten Cabacungan, Vice President and Senior Investment Strategist
Americans have spent much of the past several years telling surveys they are cautious
about the economy. Yet they continue to spend as though they are not. The University
of Michigan’s Consumer Sentiment Index remains near multidecade lows, while the
Conference Board’s Index of Consumer Confidence has also been in a yearslong downtrend.

These surveys reflect a list of persistent concerns from elevated food and gas prices to
geopolitical uncertainty and labor market anxiety.
Even so, consumer spending has remained remarkably resilient. The final estimate of Q2
real consumer spending, as measured by PCE, was revised up to an annualized rate of
3.8%, well above the 2.3% 20-year average. More recent data suggest that momentum
has continued. According to Bank of America Institute, the latest monthly total credit and

debit card spending data per household rose 6.2% year-over-year (YoY) in September, and
spending excluding gasoline was up 5.1% YoY, both the third strongest growth rates in the
past four years. 1
The persistence of consumer spending is particularly notable given that temporary cyclical
boosts like larger tax refunds are starting to fade. That raises an important question: What is
actually funding strong consumption today? Recent data suggest the drivers are becoming

increasingly more diversified, with broadening labor market trends, healthy consumer
finances and elevated wealth effects all reinforcing one another and underpinning consumers.
First, the labor market continues to provide an important foundation for consumer spending,
and recent trends suggest wage growth is supporting a broader set of households than in
recent years. Jobs growth has slowed from the exceptionally strong pace seen following the
pandemic, but broader indicators suggest labor market conditions remain relatively stable.

Although the September jobs report came in softer than expected, the three- and six-month
rolling averages indicate that the trend in jobs growth likely bottomed out last year and has
gradually improved since (Exhibit 1A). Meanwhile, the unemployment rate remains low by
historical standards and layoffs remain contained. And while wage growth has moderated
and recently trailed headline inflation (Exhibit 1B), cumulative income gains over the past
several years have likely helped many households absorb higher prices better.

An important development though has been improving wage growth trends among lower-
income households, where spending tends to be most sensitive to changes in wages. Bank
of America Institute consumer deposit account data show after-tax wage growth for lower-
income households outpaced higher-income households for a second consecutive month
in August, rising 4.7% YoY compared with 3.5% for higher-income households. Importantly,
this improvement appears to be supporting discretionary spending, not just spending

on necessities. Lower-income discretionary spending rose 5.7% YoY in August, nearly
matching the 5.9% pace for higher-income households. In other words, the narrowing gap
in wage and spending growth across income cohorts suggests consumer resilience looks
to be becoming less dependent on affluent households alone, creating a broader and more
durable foundation for consumption.
Second, household finances remain in relatively good shape, providing consumers with the

flexibility to continue spending. The ratio of household debt to disposable personal income
remains near its lowest level since the late 1990s, excluding the temporary distortions
caused by pandemic-related income support in 2020 and 2021 (Exhibit 1C). Recent
revisions to the Bureau of Economic Analysis national income accounts also suggest
consumers have a larger financial buffer than previous data suggested. Personal saving
estimates over the past year have been revised higher by $320 billion, or 39%, and the

revised savings rate remains above 4% after previous estimates showed it fell to 3% during
the summer.2 Concerns that consumers may be rapidly exhausting their savings buffers
may therefore be overstated. Supporting this view, Bank of America Institute consumer
1 Bank of America Institute, “Consumer Checkpoint: Strength in numbers.” As of October 9, 2026.
2 Bank of America, “Massive Savings Markup = Another Reason to Be Bullish.” As of October 2, 2026.

Investment Implications
Consumer spending strength
remains a key pillar of economic
and earnings growth, which keeps
us constructive on our Equity
overweight. The growing connection
between household wealth and
spending reinforces the importance
of maintaining a diversified portfolio.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
deposit account data show that middle- and lower-income households continue to maintain
deposit balances above prepandemic levels across age groups.
Third, elevated household wealth continues to provide a powerful tailwind for consumer
spending, particularly among households that account for an outsized share of overall
consumption. U.S. household net worth rose $12.8 trillion (T) to a record $195.9T in Q2,

driven largely by gains in Equities, which contributed $10.7T of the increase (Exhibit 1D).
As a result, household net worth relative to disposable income climbed to a record 8.3,
meaning households now hold more than eight dollars of wealth for every dollar of annual
disposable income. Households are also sitting on $35.8T in home equity, an all-time high
level that provides a significant balance sheet cushion. Rising asset values have helped to

support spending through what economists refer to as wealth effects. In other words, when
households feel wealthier, they tend to be more willing to spend.
For investors, the key takeaway is that consumer spending is being supported by multiple
engines despite the weaker sentiment data. Labor income trends are supporting wider
participation, healthy balance sheets are helping households absorb economic pressures,
and elevated household wealth continues to sustain higher-income spending. Together,

these forces are supportive of economic growth and corporate profits, which is particularly
important since the consumer accounts for roughly 70% of U.S. gross domestic product.
That said, it does not eliminate risk. A meaningful risk to the consumer would be a scenario
in which both the labor and wealth engines weaken simultaneously. For now, labor market
conditions remain stable, and wealth effects remain supportive. Equity market pullbacks in
recent years have done little to derail consumer spending, suggesting it would likely take a

deeper and more prolonged drawdown to alter consumer behavior.

MARKET VIEW
The Bull Market Turns Four: Older, Unloved—But Still Kicking
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
Four years ago this month, virtually no one was in the mood to buy U.S. Equities, and for
good reasons.
Rewind to October 2022: Inflation the month before clocked in at an annual rate of 8.2%.

The yield curve was inverted, a signal that has preceded most past recessions. Consumer
sentiment was abysmal, having dropped to a record low in June 2022. Recession
concerns were mounting, with an October Wall Street Journal poll survey putting the
odds of a recession within 12 months at about 63%. And realizing (belatedly) that
inflation was not “transitory,” the Fed was in the midst of administering one of the most
aggressive monetary tightening cycles in decades, raising the fed funds rate five times

between March and September 2022 by a stunning 300 basis points.
Reflecting the mood at the moment, the S&P 500 closed at 3,577 on October 12, 2022,
down 25% below its January peak, leaving investors battered and bruised but blind to the
fact that the market had bottomed. Four years on, the bull market that emerged from
the gloom of 2022 is still standing and kicking, with the S&P 500 up roughly 130% since
the October 2022 lows.
The more than doubling of the S&P over the past four years has hardly been linear—and

has been accompanied with plenty of pain and doubt. Exhibit 2 maps the rocky path
trodden by the bull over the past four years. Less than six months into the rally, the
markets were rocked by the failure of Silicon Valley Bank (SVB) on March 10, 2023. The
S&P Regional Banks Select Industry Index lost 18% that week and stoked concerns of
massive deposit runs and credit stress across the capital markets. However, owing to
emergency intervention by the government, lower yields and strength elsewhere in the

markets, the crisis passed. Some banks failed that month, but the bull market didn’t.
Exhibit 2: Revisiting the Rocky Path Trodden by the Bull.
Period "Reason to Sell" What happened instead
October 2022 Inflation >8%, aggressive Fed tightening, recession concerns Bull market begins
March 2023 SVB/regional banking crisis Stocks keep climbing
2023-24 Inverted yield curve, “recession inevitable” Economy keeps expanding
2024 “Magnificent 7* bubble,” narrow breadth, expensive valuations Earnings propel market higher

2025 Tariffs, trade war, policy uncertainty Market absorbs another shock
2026 Oil/diesel shock, 5%+ yields, Fed tightening, geopolitical risks Bull reaches fourth birthday
*Apple, Amazon, Alphabet, Nvidia, Meta, Microsoft and Tesla. Source: Chief Investment Office. As of October 9, 2026.
But recessionary concerns hung like threatening clouds over the markets for most of
2023. Aggressive Fed tightening, an inverted yield curve, and soft economic data were

all taken as harbingers of an economic slowdown and profits recession. Yet the S&P 500
continued to climb in 2023, up 26.3% for the year, driven in large part by the outsized
price performance of the Magnificent Seven.
Investors spent much of 2024 fretting about a market too dependent on technology,
and therefore a market too narrow and too expensive. The unwinding of the Japan carry
trade in August 2024 engendered more angst, as did uncertainties around the 2024

U.S. presidential election. But despite a host of misgivings, the S&P 500 delivered total
returns of 25% in 2024. The unloved bull marched on.
Total returns in 2025 were robust as well—roughly 18%—notwithstanding—yet
again—a number of headwinds. In late January, DeepSeek, a cheaper China AI model,
raised questions about America’s technology leadership and the need to spend enormous
sums on chips and data centers. The Nasdaq promptly dropped by 3.1% thereafter.

Investment Implications
The adoption phase of the AI buildout
should translate to broader earnings
growth and performance across
sectors, reinforcing our conviction
in U.S. Equities. Given a strong
fundamental backdrop, we would
continue to buy on weakness as the
bull enters its fifth year

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
January’s jolt was followed by “Liberation Day,” or the warning of punishing U.S. tariffs
on America’s major trading partners. The trade-policy shock of April 2025 caused the
S&P 500 to drop nearly 19% from its February high to its April 8 low on uncertainty
that higher import costs would decrease corporate margins and consumer spending and
upend global supply chains. Amid the trade tumult, Treasury prices fell and the U.S. dollar

weakened on concerns that foreigners were abandoning U.S. assets. Remember all the
angst over the dollar debasement trade? If that wasn’t enough, expensive valuations and
enormous capital commitments raised doubts about the AI trade and triggered talk of an
AI bubble. Yet despite this wall of worry, the S&P 500, for the third year running, posted
double-digit gains in 2025.
And double-digit gains could be in store this year, with the S&P 500 up 14% through

October 9. The bull has rumbled on, taking in stride the energy shock from the war in
the Middle East, the shift in interest rate expectations from the Fed (from rate cuts
to rate hikes), and the rising uncertainty associated with the midterm elections. These
headwinds have been countered by tailwinds such as better-than-expected corporate
earnings and better-than-anticipated real growth.
Finally, as Exhibit 3 makes clear, what has given this bull so much muscle has been

the U.S. Technology sector, specifically the Magnificent 7. Powering the bull has been
the Magnificent 7, with a staggering 344% return since the 2022 lows, while U.S.
technology has delivered a 277% return versus the S&P 500’s return of 130%. Note the
outperformance of the S&P 500 relative to the main international indices.
Looking forward: Is the bull’s greatest strength also its Achilles’ heel? Bull
markets don’t die of natural causes. And they typically don’t expire because of one

particular signal or indicator. Bull markets turn to bear markets when a cluster of signals/
metrics turn south. Think deteriorating and downward earnings revisions, higher interest
rates, widening credit spreads and tighter lending standards, growth disappointments,
stretched valuations and sentiment, deteriorating market breadth, and an external
shock. Any one of these signals could flash red and not trouble the bull.  But when they
converge or feed off each other, that’s trouble.

In addition, since a great deal of the earnings growth, market leadership and capital
expenditures (capex) spending of this bull market have come from the technology sector,
the bull’s greatest strength could become its greatest vulnerability. AI has become an
investment ecosystem unto itself, and hence the fifth year of the bull market will pivot
in part around AI revenue, frontier model adoption rates, infrastructure spending, free
cash flows, depreciation costs, debt issuance, data center utilization, cloud growth, and

competition via cheaper models and computing alternatives. To put it mildly, there is a
great deal at stake when it comes to the bull and AI.
However, this is where we stand today: We believe the bull will continue to rumble
ahead next year, with AI revenues and productivity gains justifying the costs of the AI
infrastructure. Critically, AI is moving from the buildout phase, dominated by chipmakers
and hyperscaler capex, into the adoption phase, where software firms, healthcare,

finance, energy and a myriad of other firms start to see productivity gains and margin
expansion from rising AI adoption rates. We believe this will give better breadth to the
S&P 500; so too will continued profit growth across various U.S. sectors, AI investment
that starts to pay off, and an economy capable of absorbing setbacks or periodic bouts
of uncertainty.
The path forward won’t be linear—and there will be fits and starts, and market
drawdowns. That’s normal for any market. But that aside, the bull will keep rumbling,

roaming and kicking in 2027.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What is our view of the macro and market landscape heading
into 2027 and beyond?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Office
A supply side-led economic expansion continues, driven by capital investment in the AI
buildout, solid consumer spending and fiscal tailwinds still blowing, albeit with less force.
This creates a backdrop that keeps nominal growth well-above trend and, importantly,

above the interest cost on national debt.
The interest rate backdrop largely remains the same as of today, but, instead of the
sharp increases that we experienced in 2026, the curve should settle down, and yields
should remain relatively range bound. The Fed is likely to tap on the breaks a couple of
more times before reassessing new inflation, growth and employment trends later in
2027.
Concerns surrounding credit risk and "circular" financing are likely to rise the further we

move into the AI buildout, but we do not expect a default cycle to develop. Rather, we
expect the sharp backup in yields to place pressure namely on lower-quality credits in
areas having a difficult time building the cash flow necessary to pay the higher interest
costs.
We expect a broadening out within the market but technology to still maintain its
heavyweight positioning. As we move from training and evolving the large language
models to agentic, and physical AI new leaders are likely to develop. In addition, we

anticipate AI adopters such as biotechnology, transport, logistics, advance manufacturing
and financial companies to enter new growth and productivity phases.
Lastly, overall earnings growth in the U.S. should be low to mid double-digit percent
stages in 2027, which is about half of the growth from 2026 thus far but still around
double from normal levels.
Credit risks developing and higher energy prices for longer are the main risks to our bull
case for Equities extending further into 2027 and beyond.

Index Definitions
ecurities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

University of Michigan’s Consumer Sentiment Index is a monthly survey of consumer confidence levels in the United States conducted by the University of Michigan.
Conference Board’s Index of Consumer Confidence measures what consumers are feeling about their expected financial situation, whether that's optimistic or pessimistic.
Consumer Price Index is a statistical measure that tracks the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a representative "market basket" of everyday goods and

services.
Nasdaq Composite Index is a stock market measure that tracks the performance of more than 3,000 stocks listed on the Nasdaq exchange.

### Page 1

Circle 盘前涨逾 3% ，公司第三季度业绩全⾯超预期，总营收及储备收⼊达 8.65 亿美元，同
⽐增⻓ 17% ；调整后 EBITDA 同⽐⼤增 85% ⾄ 3.17 亿美元， ARC 代币预售贡献 1.82 亿美元
⼀次性收⼊。利率下⾏拖累核⼼储备收⼊，但 USDC 链上交易量同⽐增⻓ 49% 。
摘要：
稳定币发⾏商 Circle Internet Group 业绩全⾯超越市场预期， ARC 代币预售带来的⼀次性收⼊推
动调整后盈利能⼒⼤幅提升，股价盘前⾛⾼逾 3% 。
Circle 周三公布 2026 年第三季度财报，总营收及储备收⼊达 8.65 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 17% ，超出市
场预期的 7.767 亿美元；调整后 EBITDA 为 3.17 亿美元，同⽐⼤增 85% ，远超预期的 2.028 亿美
元；净利润 2.26 亿美元，亦显著⾼于市场预期的 9160 万美元。公司同步维持 2026 年全年业绩指
引不变。
本季度业绩亮点在很⼤程度上受益于 Arc 区块链主⽹上线所带来的 ARC 代币预售收⼊。这⼀⾮经

常性收⼊来源掩盖了储备收⼊下滑的压⼒ —— 后者因利率⾛低⽽同⽐下降 8% 。与此同时，
USDC 链上交易量同⽐⼤幅增⻓ 49%，显⽰出核⼼稳定币业务的强劲使⽤动能。
ARC 代币预售成为本季最⼤业绩变量
其他收⼊是本季最具弹性的收⼊来源，同⽐增⻓ 627% ⾄ 2.07 亿美元，其中 1.82 亿美元来⾃ ARC
代币预售。
Circle 于 9 ⽉ 16 ⽇正式启动 Arc 区块链公共主⽹，这是该公司⾃ USDC 发⾏以来规模最⼤的产品发
布，⾸⽇即有逾 100 家机构及⽣态建设者接⼊。与此同时， Circle 完成了 ARC 代币的创世铸造，
总供应量为 100 亿枚，成为⾸家为全新 Layer-1 区块链铸造⽹络代币的上市公司。 ARC 代币旨在
作为 Arc ⽹络安全、实⽤功能及治理的原⽣协调机制，⽹络计划于 2027 年完成向权益证明
（ Proof of Stake ）机制的过渡。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

Arc 的全球开发者社区⽬前拥有逾 75,000 名 Arc House 成员及 10,000 名 Architect ⼤使，后者在私
有主⽹期间共构建了逾 1,200 个项⽬。
储备收⼊承压，利率下⾏侵蚀核⼼利润
Circle 的储备收⼊为 6.58 亿美元，同⽐下降 8% ，主要原因是储备回报率下滑 61 个基点⾄ 3.5% ，
部分被 USDC 平均流通量同⽐增⻓ 8% ⾄ 733 亿美元所抵消。
从季度环⽐来看，储备收⼊已连续两个季度承压 ——2025 年第四季度储备收⼊为 7.33 亿美元，
此后逐季下滑，本季度降⾄ 6.58 亿美元。这⼀趋势反映出利率环境对 Circle 核⼼盈利模式的持续
影响。
分销成本的改善在⼀定程度上缓和了上述压⼒。本季度总分销、交易及其他成本为 4.12 亿美元，
同⽐下降 8% ，主要得益于分销付款减少，由此带动收⼊减去分销成本后的 RLDC 利润率升⾄
52% ，同⽐提升约 13 个百分点。
USDC 使⽤量加速扩张，机构合作密集落地
USDC 链上交易量本季度达到 13.9 万亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 49% ，链上资产规模较去年同期增⻓

36% 。持有超过 10 美元 USDC 的 " 有效钱包 " 数量达 730 万个，同⽐增⻓ 16% 。期末 USDC 稳定币
市场份额为 27% 。
本季度，围绕 USDC 的机构合作密集落地。 Visa 将其 Visa Direct 稳定币预充值业务从试点转⼊商
业部署，通过 Inyo Global ⽀持客⼾以 USDC 在逾 180 个国家和地区实现准实时跨境⽀付结算。
SAP ⽣态系公司 Tereina 宣布将 USDC 和 EURC 整合进其企业⽀付基础设施，涉及覆盖全球 84%
商业交易的 SAP ⽣态系统。三星钱包则已允许符合条件的美国 Galaxy ⽤⼾通过 USDC 向逾 60 个
国家的银⾏账⼾汇款。
此外，币安宣布对 Circle 进⾏ 1 亿美元股权投资，并扩⼤与 Circle 的战略合作伙伴关系，重点⾯向
新兴市场推⼴ USDC 。 Circle ⾸席执⾏官 Jeremy Allaire 表⽰， " 机构正在基于 USDC 进⾏构建和
扩展，这⼀趋势在本季度持续得到印证。 "

收购 Tazapay ，布局跨境⽀付新赛道
Circle ⽀付⽹络（ CPN ）本季度年化⽀付总额达 413 亿美元。为进⼀步拓展⽀付基础设施，
Circle 与新加坡跨境⽀付平台 Tazapay 签署最终收购协议，预计于 2027 年完成交割，届时仍需监
管部⻔批准。
Tazapay 年化⽀付规模逾 250 亿美元，拥有逾 60 家银⾏及⾦融科技合作伙伴，本地出⾦渠道覆盖
逾 100 个市场，且其约 60% 的交易量已涉及稳定币。 Circle 表⽰，此次收购将强化其在亚太、拉
美及新兴市场的⽀付发起与结算能⼒，⽽这些市场对 USDC 计价交易的需求正在增⻓。
裁员约 5% ，全年指引维持不变
本季度， Circle 完成约 5% 的裁员，以优化组织架构与经营模式的匹配度。与此次重组相关的费
⽤约为 915 万美元，主要包括通知期薪资、遣散费及员⼯福利⽀出。
尽管进⾏⼈员调整，公司仍维持 2026 年全年财务指引不变，包括：其他收⼊ 3.10 亿⾄ 3.30 亿美
元（含 ARC 代币预售已确认收⼊）； RLDC 利润率 41.7% ⾄ 43.7% ；调整后运营⽀出 5.70 亿⾄

5.85 亿美元。公司同时维持 USDC 流通量多年周期内 40% 复合年增⻓率的中⻓期指引。

Document 341: Following the oil shock, El Niño threatens to hurt macroeconomic resilience in Sub-Saharan Africa further (2026-10-21)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Following the oil shock, El Niño threatens to hurt macroeconomic resilience in Sub-Saharan Africa further (2026-10-21).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

21 October 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
FOLLOWING THE OIL SHOCK, EL NIÑO THREATENS TO HURT MACROECONOMIC RESILIENCE IN SUB-SAHARAN
AFRICA FURTHER
Lucas Plé
Seven months after the start of the war in the Middle East, which has led to a sharp rise in oil prices, sub-Saharan Africa is holding up well
overall. The deterioration in external accounts and the rise in inflation are more contained than in 2022, during the last oil shock. However,
this resilience is fragile and is eroding as the conflict drags on. Furthermore, an exceptional El Niño event could significantly worsen the

macroeconomic environment in 2027 1.
Economic growth remains robust
For countries with available data, GDP growth held up well in the first half of 2026 2. In mid-October, the IMF revised its regional forecast for
the full year upwards to 4.5% (+0.2 percentage points compared with April), in line with the performance for 2025.
1 El Niño 2026–2027: multiple pressures on prices and supplies
2 1.4% year-on-year (y/y) in South Africa, 4.2% in Nigeria, 5.1% in Kenya, 7% in Angola, 7.3% in Ghana.

Sub-Saharan Africa is highly vulnerable to climate shocks via the agricultural commodities channel

### Page 2

2
The bank for
a changing
world
Chart of the Week / 21 October 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
A mitigated shock on terms of trade
High prices for export commodities (gold, copper, platinum) have helped to cushion the impact on terms of trade and the external
accounts of hydrocarbon-importing countries. For the 10 countries in our sample (see heatmap), the depreciation of their currencies
against the US dollar since last February has been moderate overall (2.3% on average).

Inflation has started to rise again, but it is still being contained so far
In the ten countries in our sample, although inflation is rising again, it is lower year-on-year for the first eight months of 2026 than it was
for the same period in 2022. This is due to:
1. Monetary policies that were still restrictive before the conflict started;
2. Fuel subsidies;
3.   Macroeconomic reforms implemented in recent years (a return to orthodox monetary policy in Nigeria, and efforts towards fiscal

consolidation in many countries);
4. Moderate currency depreciation since February.
As a result, few central banks have tightened their monetary policy since February (South Africa, Uganda and Tanzania).
However, this resilience is still fragile…
On the one hand, governments that have chosen to cap fuel price rises are seeing their public finances deteriorate. In Senegal, energy
subsidies will go above 3% of GDP in 2026, which is unsustainable. On the other hand, the region’s external accounts are exposed to

volatile global commodity prices and tighter international financing conditions.
… and the macroeconomic environment could quickly worsen in 2027, due to an exceptional El Niño event
The region is particularly vulnerable to climate shocks via the agricultural commodities channel. Given agriculture’s high share in its GDP ,
sub-Saharan Africa is more exposed to downside risks to growth than Latin America, Asia or Central Europe (1 st column of the heatmap).

It is also more exposed than other regions of the world to the inflationary effect of rising food prices, given their higher share in the
consumer basket (2 nd column). Finally, even though its dependence on food imports is lower than in Asia and Europe (3 rd column), the
region is highly vulnerable to rising international food commodity prices due to its fragile external accounts. For several countries in our
sample, foreign exchange reserves cover less than four months’ worth of imports of goods and services (Uganda, Tanzania, Ghana and

Zambia), leaving little room for manoeuvre for absorbing a further external shock.
In addition to shocks via the agricultural commodities channel, other localised risks must be considered. In Southern Africa, El Niño is
expected to intensify droughts and threaten electricity generation as a result, particularly in Zambia (where hydropower accounts
for nearly 80% of domestic electricity generation). In East Africa, the increased flooding that El Niño is expected to cause will threaten

infrastructure – particularly logistics infrastructure – which is already less efficient than the global average 3. These two sources of
vulnerability could also increase pressure on growth, prices and the external accounts.
In conclusion, if oil prices remain high and El Niño is exceptionally severe, the region will struggle to absorb this double shock in 2027.
Despite their status as net exporters of hydrocarbons, Nigeria and Angola are also exposed to a deterioration in the macroeconomic

environment in 2027. Only South Africa appears to be better positioned to absorb the shock.
lucas.ple@bnpparibas.com
3 See the World Bank’s Logistics Performance Index.

设实施临时暂停令，令相关股票雪上加霜。与此同时，原本被市场视为在共和党国会畅⾏⽆阻

的 1.5 万亿美元国防预算扩张预期亦随之落空。⺠主党掌控国会将使过去两年共和党主导下的监
管宽松路径出现逆转， AI 基础设施投资的政策红利⾯临重新评估，⽽清洁能源、医疗保障等此前
受压板块则将获得更强的⽴法⽀撑。
然⽽，科技板块内部出现明显分裂。在 AI 数据中⼼运营商（ Neocloud ）⼤跌之际，云⼤⼚
（ Hyperscalers ，超⼤规模云服务商）逆势暴涨，微软涨、亚⻢逊均涨超 6% 。资⾦从依赖新建
算⼒的标的出逃，同时涌⼊⼿握现成算⼒的超⼤规模云⼚商。市场认为若数据中⼼新建放缓，
现有资产的稀缺价值上升， AI 资本开⽀被抑制，利好超⼤规模云⼚商。
⾼盛交易台的约翰 · 弗勒德（ John Flood ）点评称：
德州易主：数据中⼼运营商遭⾎洗
数据中⼼运营商是这场选举最直接的受害者。
Hut 8 跌近 23% ， Cipher Digital 重挫 21% ， Core Scientific 和 IREN 跌幅均超 10% ， Applied
Digital 下跌 6.7% ⾄ 8.8% ， CoreWeave 下跌约 9.5% 。作为数据中⼼建设最⼤受益者，英伟达股

价当⽇⼤跌 3.17% 。
⽬前我们交易台最突出的主题是买⼊超⼤规模云⼚商（ Hyperscalers ），同时卖出半导体
（ Semis ）。为什么要买⼊超⼤规模云⼚商？如果由于⽆法建设更多产能导致算⼒变得更加稀
缺，你就可以以更⾼的价格出售它。如果你⽆法建设更多产能，你的资本⽀出就会减少。如果不
把现⾦⽤于产能建设，你就极有可能利⽤这些闲置资⾦重新开始回购股票。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

打击来⾃⼀个具体的地名：德克萨斯州。
根据研究机构 SemiAnalysis 数据，德州占美国 2030 年预期上线算⼒约四分之⼀，多家机构预估
⼤量德州规划的数据中⼼产能⾯临搁置或延期⻛险。⾏业测算显⽰， 2027 年约两成超⼤规模云
⼚商资本开⽀对应的前期建设⼯作存在不确定性， 2028 年新增产能受冲击⽐例更⾼。
⺠主党候选⼈ Gina Hinojosa 赢得德州州⻓选举，成为逾 30 年来该州⾸位⺠主党籍州⻓，其竞选
纲领明确主张对数据中⼼建设实施临时暂停令。瑞银全球宏观股票策略及美国股票策略研究主
管 Keith Parker 在给客⼾的报告中写道，出⼝⺠调显⽰，选⺠强烈反对在其社区新建数据中⼼。
Clear Street 颠覆性技术股权研究董事总经理 Brian Dobson 指出：
为数据中⼼建设提供电⼒与设备⽀持的企业同样承压。 GE Vernova 下跌 11.5% ，在德州数据中
⼼项⽬敞⼝最⼤的两家电⼒公司 Constellation Energy 和 Vistra 分别下跌 9.2% 和 15.4% 。

Siebert Financial ⾸席投资官 Mark Malek 表⽰：
分析认为，不会出现数据中⼼全⾯关停，但会迎来更多审批繁⽂缛节，⼤量规划项⽬延后，进
⼊建设慢、周期拉⻓的状态。多头甚⾄提出企业会把资本开⽀前置到 2027 年抢电⼒与许可，但
市场多数参与者认为半导体板块尚未充分定价这⼀轮周期下⾏压⼒。
这股 ' 蓝⾊浪潮 ' 将对股市产⽣影响，短期内最⼤的影响可能来⾃州层⾯对数据中⼼监管的加强。
" 德克萨斯州政治格局的转变可能带来额外的数据中⼼监管措施，这将有利于拥有良好企业公⺠
记录的⽼牌机构。 "
"AI 基础设施建设不会消失，只会变得更贵。税收优惠减少、电⼒和⽔资源成本责任增加，将压
缩回报空间，令利润率对投资者⽽⾔愈发关键。最脆弱的 AI 企业，是那些⾼度依赖融资的公司，
⽽⾮规模最⼤的资本⽀出⽅。 "

### Page 3

Evercore ISI 分析师团队在研究报告中指出：“ 若数据中⼼开发环境趋于收紧，将对 AI 基础设施
相关股票带来更多负⾯情绪。 ”
也有声⾳认为抛售过度。 LO:TECH 研究主管 Adam McCarthy 指出，特朗普政府⼀贯强⼒⽀持 AI
发展，针对数据中⼼概念股的⼤幅下跌看似反应过度，已建成并完成转型改造的矿机基础设
施，在监管收紧的环境下反⽽更具价值。
建得越少反⽽越值钱：云⼤⼚单⽇暴增万亿市值
截⾄周三收盘，微软涨 6.7% ，亚⻢逊涨 6.1% ，⾕歌⺟公司 Alphabet 涨 3.2% ， Meta 涨 1.4% ，苹
果涨 5.3% 。五⼤科技巨头单⽇市值合计增加近 1 万亿美元。
市场逻辑在于：若数据中⼼新建步伐放缓，现有资产的稀缺价值将随之上升，同时有望抑制⼤
型科技公司不断膨胀的 AI 资本⽀出。投资者此前持续因举债加码 AI 投资、回报前景不明确⽽惩罚
这些股票。
B. Riley Wealth ⾸席市场策略师 Art Hogan 表⽰：
Dakota Wealth Management ⾼级投资组合经理 Robert Pavlik 说得更直⽩：

这句话触及了问题的核⼼。近期，亚⻢逊、微软、 Alphabet 和 Meta 的股价持续承压，原因是亚
⻢逊、 Alphabet 和 Meta 的⾃由现⾦流已转为负值 —— 资本开⽀超过了经营产⽣的现⾦，这些公
司不得不依靠发债和股权融资来⽀撑投⼊。Pavlik 进⼀步指出：
超⼤规模云⼚商已拥有现成的数据中⼼，数据中⼼不再被⼤量新建，意味着这些资产更具价值。
建设节奏放缓，资本⽀出的步伐也会随之趋缓，这对超⼤规模云⼚商⽽⾔是确定性的正⾯影响。
市场表现得就好像这预⽰着 AI 基建热潮的终结。如果这意味着超⼤规模云服务商不再砸下天⽂数
字般的资⾦去扩建算⼒，那么它们的⾃由现⾦流有望重新转正。

### Page 4

除了存量资产增值，市场还预期⼤⼚可以把算⼒建设向⻢来西亚等海外地区转移，对冲美国本
⼟审批阻⼒，进⼀步强化其相对于新兴云⼚商的竞争壁垒。
⻢斯克相关资产：政治⻛险溢价显现
特斯拉下跌 3.3% ， SpaceX 股价下跌 3.5% ⾄ 8 ⽉中旬以来低位。⻢斯克此前投⼊近 5 亿美元⽀持
共和党候选⼈，其中包括帮助共和党维持对德克萨斯州控制权的相关⾏动，如今其政治对⼿重
夺国会控制权。
XTB 研究总监 Kathleen Brooks 表⽰，此次选举结果令外界对 SpaceX 与特斯拉潜在合并计划产⽣
质疑。
特朗普概念加密资产全线下挫
据 CoinGecko 数据，特朗普联合创办的 World Liberty Financial 旗下 WLFI 代币过去 24 ⼩时跌幅约
7% ，以其名字命名的 Meme 币跌幅约 17% 。
World Liberty Financial 发⾔⼈ David Wachsman 则表⽰，该公司 " ⾃成⽴以来，始终遵守所有适
⽤的美国法律法规 " ，并称 " 国会若⽆端针对⼀家致⼒于扩⼤美元普及的合规公司，令⼈担忧 " 。

超⼤规模云服务商此前陷⼊两难：⼀⽅⾯因⼤⼿笔开⽀⽽受到惩罚，另⼀⽅⾯若缩减开⽀同样会
招来质疑。从某种意义上说，这场选举是送给超⼤规模云服务商的⼀份礼物 —— 给它们的⽀出踩
下刹⻋的，不是企业⾃⼰，⽽是政府。
" 国会虽⽆法阻⽌合并，但可通过加强审查的⽅式拖延进程。
⻢斯克为共和党投⼊了数亿美元竞选资⾦，如今其政治对⼿重夺国会控制权，与⻢斯克相关的股
票可能持续承压。 "

### Page 5

加密资产研究通讯《 Crypto is Macro Now 》作者 Noelle Acheson 指出，此轮跌势 " 既反映了相关
资产光环的消退，也折射出市场对潜在调查或司法追诉的预期 " 。
同时，特朗普媒体与科技集团股价⼀度重挫 17% ，创历史新低；追踪该股的杠杆 ETF 产品 DJTU
跌幅达 32% 。
国防承包商：预算扩张预期受挫
国防板块普跌，诺思罗普 · 格鲁曼（ Northrop Grumman ）下跌 3.7% ， RTX 、洛克希德 · ⻢丁
（ Lockheed Martin ）和 L3Harris 跌幅均在 3% ⾄ 6% 之间。
此前市场预期特朗普提出的 1.5 万亿美元国防预算在共和党控制的国会将畅⾏⽆阻，但⺠主党接
掌国会令这⼀路径⽣变。
eToro 全球市场策略师 Lale Akoner 表⽰：
清洁能源：政策预期驱动反弹
清洁能源板块全线⾛⾼。
First Solar 涨 8.4% ，居家太阳能安装商 Sunrun 涨 6.9% ，逆变器制造商 Enphase Energy 涨

8.8% ，清洁能源开发商 NextEra Energy 涨 4.6% 。欧洲市场同步反应，丹⻨ Vestas 涨 2.9% ，
Orsted 涨 4.6% ，葡萄⽛ EDP Renewables 涨 2.8% 。
投资者押注⺠主党掌控国会将为清洁能源提供更强有⼒的联邦政策⽀撑，同时增加对推翻排放
法规相关努⼒的阻⼒。⺠主党还可能借助《国会审查法》叫停放宽燃油⻋销售限制的相关法
规。
Invesco EMEA 全球市场策略师 Paul Jackson 表⽰， " 油⽓价格上涨越多，推⼴清洁能源的经济
动⼒就越强。 "
医疗上涨、私营监狱下跌：政策⽅向决定股价
医疗板块受益于政策预期转向。医院运营商 HCA Healthcare 和 Universal Health Services 分别上
涨 8.5% 和 6.8% ，保险商 Centene 和 Molina Healthcare 分别上涨 7.3% 和 6.3% 。
摩根⼠丹利分析师在研报中指出：
" 艰难的债务上限谈判可能推迟⽀出增加，更依赖新政府合同的企业将⽐合同基础稳固的企业⾯

临更⼤的⻛险。真正的⻛险在于，⽀出增速低于预期，从⽽引发价格修正。 "
" 此次选举结果将为国会推进⺠主党医疗政策优先项提供更⼤空间，包括恢复《平价医疗法案》
强化补贴，或逆转共和党医疗补助削减措施中的就业要求规定。
尽管我们仍认为在下次总统⼤选周期前出现实质性医疗政策变化的可能性较低，但选举结果可能
改变政策叙事⾛向。 "

Source PDF: all report pdf/USA_ Pushing Back Fed Rate Hike on Lower Core PCE Inflation; Q2 GDP Revised Up to +2.2%; Trade Deficit Widens More Than Expected; Lowering Q3 GDP Tracking to +3.3%.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 8

### Page 2

forecast +3.4%, prior revised +0.7%

MAIN POINTS:
1. The core PCE index increased +0.25% in August, somewhat below expectations, and
the year-over-year rate increased 3.01%. We estimate that the Dallas Fed’s trimmed
mean PCE measure increased 0.16% month over month and 2.20% year over year.
2. Year-over-year core PCE was revised downward 0.36pp in July to 2.98%, primarily
reflecting the methodological revisions to the calculation of the portfolio management
(-0.23pp), computer accessories and software (-0.11pp), and legal services (+0.01pp)

components of PCE. These downward revisions were larger than we expected, mostly
reflecting a larger revision to the portfolio management component than we expected.
3. Today’s inflation report, especially the downward revisions resulting from the
methodological changes, imply that core PCE inflation is likely to substantially
undershoot the median FOMC participant’s forecast of 3.4% for 2026 on a Q4/Q4 basis.
We now expect a 3.0% increase for core PCE in 2026 on a Q4/Q4 basis. Coupled with

comments from New York Fed President John Williams yesterday that “with the policy
action we took at our September meeting, there is no need for urgency, and we have
time to gather more information,” we now think that an October hike is unlikely.  We are
pushing back the second hike in our forecast to December, and we see a strong chance
that the FOMC will ultimately conclude that additional rate hikes are unnecessary.
4. Real GDP was revised up by 0.7pp to +2.2% in Q2 (quarter-over-quarter annualized),

largely reflecting upward revisions to consumption (+0.4pp to +3.8%) and investment
(+1.9pp to +4.6%) growth. The strength in consumer spending in Q2 likely continued to
reflect the boost to consumer cashflow from larger-than-usual tax refunds, but we
continue to expect consumer spending growth to slow somewhat in 2026Q4 as the fiscal
support fades and the headwind from higher energy prices remains. Stronger
investment growth reflected an upward revision to private fixed investment (+0.5pp to

+9.0%), driven by data center investment in the nonresidential structures category
(+1.9pp to +0.1%), and an upward revision to the contribution from inventories (+0.2pp
to -0.5pp).
5. Reflecting today’s annual revisions to the national accounts, real GDP was revised up
by 0.4pp to +2.5% in Q1 (quarter-over-quarter annualized), largely reflecting upward
revisions to the contributions from consumption and exports. Revisions for prior years
were modest: real GDP growth on a Q4/Q4 basis was unrevised for 2025 (+2.0%) and

2023 (+3.4%), and slightly revised down for 2024 (-0.2pp to +2.4%) and up for 2022
30 September 2026   2
Goldman Sachs

### Page 3

(+0.1pp to +1.3%). 
6. Personal income increased 0.2% in August, below expectations. Year-over-year 
disposable personal income growth reached 4.8% in August. Personal spending rose 
0.9% in August, in line with consensus expectations. The savings rate decreased 0.5pp to 
4.1% in August, after a large 1.6pp upward revision to the savings rate last month driven 
by an upward revision to the level of disposable personal income (+1.2pp contribution) 
and a downward revision to personal spending as a share of DPI (+0.4pp). 
7. According to the Advance Economic Indicators Report, the goods trade deficit 
widened by $13.7bn to $132.6bn (seasonally adjusted) in August, well above 
expectations. Goods exports increased by $3.7bn to $203.4bn, largely driven by an 
increase in industrial supplies exports. Goods imports increased by $17.4bn to 
$336.1bn, driven by a large increase in industrial supplies and capital goods imports. 
8. According to the ADP report, private sector employment rose by 90k in September 
(month-over-month, seasonally adjusted), slightly above consensus expectations. 
Employment in the services sector rose 59k, primarily reflecting a 55k increase in 
education and health services and a 22k increase in leisure and hospitality employment 
that were partly offset by a 16k decrease in financial activities employment. 
Employment in goods-producing industries increased by 31k, driven by a 17k increase in 
manufacturing and a 15k increase in construction employment. ADP’s new median 
annual base pay measure, which better tracks other measures of wage growth than its 
gross pay measure, increased 3.0% for job stayers, unchanged from the August pace, 
and 4.8% for job changers, 0.1pp above the August pace. We left our forecast for August 
nonfarm payroll growth unchanged at +80k (vs. +90k consensus) ahead of Friday’s 
release. 
9. The details of the personal income and spending report were firmer than our previous 
GDP tracking assumptions. While the trade-related details of the Advance Economic 
Indicators report were much weaker than our previous assumptions, suggesting notably 
stronger imports growth in Q3 than we previously assumed, the inventories details were 
significantly stronger. On net, we lowered our Q3 GDP tracking estimate by 0.1pp to 
+3.3% (quarter-over-quarter annualized).

紀彥呈 / 資訊軟體、半導體  
+886-2-2326-9899#1940 / ianchi@cathayfut.com.tw

聯電 2303 TT  ( 電子–半導體 ) 
聯年攀升，價量齊揚 
投資與評價 
1. 聯電 3Q26 受惠成熟製程及 AI 相關需求回升，晶圓出貨季增 7.3%，稼
動率攀升至 92%，本業創近三年新高；4Q26 隨新加坡 Fab 12i 產能持
續開出，22nm 新產品及矽光子業務逐步貢獻，稼動率維持 90%以上。 
2. 聯電 26 年受惠成熟製程需求回升 、 新加坡Fab 12i 產能開出及稼動率提
升，EPS 7.33 元；27 年成熟製程供需趨緊，加上矽光子、TFLN、DTC
及先進封裝等高附加價值業務逐步貢獻 ，EPS 8.35 元， 營運創歷史新高。 
3. 目前聯電交易在 128 元，位於近三年參考本益比區間 9~22x 平均值位
置，考量成熟製程復甦加快，稼動率攀升，且先進封裝與矽光子將開始
貢獻營收，維持買進投資建議，因上調27 年獲利預估，本益比由平均值
之 16 倍調高至中間偏上之 18 倍，目標價由 130 元(16X 修正前 27(F) 
PER)，調升至 150 元(18X 修正後 27(F) PER)。 
重點更新 
成熟製程需求提升使聯電 3Q26 稼動率達 92%，本業創三近年新高 
聯電 3Q26 受惠於成熟製程供需結構持續改善，AI 相關 PMIC、通訊與消費
性電子需求回升 ， 整體產能利用率由2Q26 的 85%提升至 92%，但因漲價幅
度略低於原先預期，聯電 3Q26 營收 742.39 億元(+8.0% QoQ) ，略低於預
期。隨著產能利用率提升 ， 毛利率由前一季之32.5%提升至 34.0%(+1.5ppts 
QoQ)，營業利益 186.43 億元(+29.3% QoQ) ，惟 2Q26 因權益法認列較高
投資收益，3Q26 業外收支淨額由前一季之 302.36 億元降至 12.68 億元，稅
後淨利為 163.89 億元(-61.2% QoQ)，EPS 1.32 元，本業為三近年新高。 
預估聯電 4Q26 AI 需求支撐晶圓出貨成長，產能利用率維持九成以上 
隨著 AI 相關電源管理、FPGA 及高速連接晶片需求持續成長，加上成熟製程
供需逐步趨緊 ， 新加坡Fab 12i 產能持續擴充 ， 配合22nm 新產品逐步量產，
以及矽光子相關業務開始貢獻營收，但因 3Q26 產品價格調漲幅度低於預
期，故下修 4Q26 混合 ASP 預估，小幅下調聯電 4Q26 營收 1.5%至 777.49
億元(+4.7% QoQ)。儘管部分 PC 相關 LCD 控制 IC、DDIC 及 Wi-Fi 需求出
現放緩，但 AI 相關高附加價值產品占比提升，維持產能利用率 90%出頭之
預估，惟混合 ASP 調低影響毛利率表現，調降毛利率預估至 34.6%。下調稅
後淨利至 166.90 億元(+1.8% QoQ)，EPS 1.34 元。

### Page 2

報告內容 
成熟製程需求提升使聯電 3Q26 稼動率達 92%，本業創近三年新高 
聯電受惠於成熟製程供需結構持續改善，以及AI 相關電源管理、通訊與消費性電子需求回升，
3Q26 晶圓出貨量達 121.2 萬片 12 吋約當晶圓(+7.3% QoQ)， 創歷史新高 ， 整體產能利用率由
2Q26 的 85%提升至 92%，其中多座 12 吋晶圓廠已達滿載，8 吋產線亦隨客戶訂單回流而明
顯復甦 。 此外22/28nm 製程營收再創單季新高 ， 其中22nm 營收占晶圓銷售比重提升至 18.3%，
主要受惠於 DDIC、車用雷達及 Wi-Fi IC 等新產品持續導入，帶動晶圓出貨成長，本季針對非
長約客戶有進行部分漲價，推算平均漲價幅度約 5~6%，但由於 8 吋的產能利用率提升幅度大
於 12 吋，且 8 吋的 ASP 低於 12 吋，使得本季的混合 ASP 持平於前一季。 
因漲價幅度略低於原先預期，聯電 3Q26 營收 742.39 億元(+8.0% QoQ) ，略低於預期。隨著
產能利用率提升 ， 有效分攤晶圓製造固定成本 ， 且高附加價值22nm 及特殊製程產品持續增加，
抵銷部分折舊費用增加影響，毛利率由前一季之 32.5%提升至 34.0%(+1.5ppts QoQ)。在管理
及研發費用季減，以及其他營業收益增加帶動下，營業費用降至65.62 億元(-17.0% QoQ)，推
升營業利益至 186.43 億元(+29.3% QoQ)，營業利益率 25.1%。惟 2Q26 因權益法認列較高投
資收益 ， 使業外收支基期偏高 ，3Q26 營業外收支淨額由前一季之 302.36 億元降至 12.68 億元，
所得稅費用增加，稅後淨利為 163.89 億元(-61.2% QoQ)，EPS 1.32 元，本業為近三年新高。

預估聯電 4Q26 AI 需求支撐晶圓出貨成長，產能利用率維持九成以上 
隨著 AI 相關電源管理、FPGA 及高速連接晶片需求持續成長，加上成熟製程供需逐步趨緊，預
期聯電 4Q26 晶圓出貨量將維持低個位數季增 ， 美元計價ASP 亦保持穩定 。 此外新加坡Fab 12i
產能持續擴充 ， 帶動整體季度產能由132.5 萬片提升至 133.7 萬片 12 吋約當晶圓 ， 配合22nm
新產品逐步量產 ， 以及矽光子相關業務開始貢獻營收 ，但因 3Q26 產品價格調漲幅度低於預期，
故下修 4Q26 混合ASP 預估 ， 小幅下調聯電4Q26 營收預估1.5%至 777.49 億元(+4.7% QoQ)。

### Page 3

儘管部分 PC 相關 LCD 控制 IC、DDIC 及 Wi-Fi 需求出現放緩，但AI 相關高附加價值產品占比
提升，維持九成以上產能利用率之預估，惟混合 ASP 調低影響毛利率表現，調降毛利率預估
2.1 個百分點至 34.6%。下修營業利益預估 9.4%至 182.82 億元(-1.9% QoQ)， 營業利益率23.5%，
稅後淨利 166.90 億元(+1.8% QoQ)，EPS 1.34 元。

資料來源: UMC、國泰證期研究部整理

晶圓出貨與稼動率雙升，預期聯電 26 年營運創歷史新高 
聯電 26 年受惠於成熟製程供需環境改善，AI 相關需求持續帶動電源管理、FPGA 及高速連接
晶片成長，配合 22/28nm 製程在 DDIC、車用雷達及 Wi-Fi 等應用的新產品持續導入，相較
25 年產能利用率受消費性電子需求疲弱及成熟製程庫存調整影響，26 年自第二季起晶圓出貨
逐季回升，整體產能利用率由 1Q26 的 79%提升至 3Q26 的 92%，第四季亦預期維持 90%以
上，其中 22nm 製程營收占比持續提升，顯示高價值製程需求逐步增強。此外聯電積極布局矽
光子、TFLN 及先進封裝等新技術，26 年已與 SILITH 達成矽光子產品量產里程碑，並與國內其
他廠商合作開發 AI 伺服器應用 DTC 深溝槽電容，相關技術逐步由產品驗證轉向商業化。 
聯電新加坡 Fab 12i 產能也逐步開出，與 Intel 合作之 12nm 專案同樣進展順利，預計 26 年提
供 PDK 與 IP，應用涵蓋 Wi-Fi、數位電視與高速介面，惟推算 3Q26 產品價格調漲幅度低於原
先預期，小幅下修 26 年營收預估 1.2%至 2,817.58 億元(+18.6% YoY)。因 3Q26 混合 ASP 低
於預期及下調 4Q26 混合 ASP 預估 ， 小幅調低毛利率預估 1.3 個百分點至 32.7%(+3.7ppts YoY)。
調降營業利益預估 3.9%至 620.62 億元(+47.9% YoY)， 稅後淨利914.20 億元(+120.1% YoY)，
EPS 7.33 元，營運創歷史新高。

### Page 4

預期聯電 27 年 ASP 調漲與產品組合優化，推升營運再創新高 
預期聯電 27 年主要成長動能將由 26 年之出貨量與產能利用率提升逐步轉向 ASP 調整及高附
加價值產品組合改善，隨著 AI 基礎建設持續擴大，電源管理 IC、FPGA 及高速連接晶片需求維
持強勁，加上部分 8 吋及 12 吋製程已接近滿載，成熟製程供需結構持續趨緊，有利聯電與客
戶進行價格協商，預估 1Q27 混合 ASP 季增 7~8%。22nm 製程持續受惠於客戶新產品量產及
晶圓代工滲透率提升，矽光子則隨 12 吋 PIC 平台支援 1.6T 光通訊應用，以及 TFLN 高速調變
器與先進封裝整合技術逐步成熟，開始增加營收貢獻。雖然新增矽光子、DTC 及 TFLN 產能主
要於 28 年底後陸續開出 ， 使27 年晶圓出貨成長受到既有產能限制 ， 但預期透過製程瓶頸改善、
既有產能重新配置，以及高附加價值產品比重提升，仍可持續推升營運規模。 
上修 27 年營收預估 0.3%至 3,689.23 億元(+30.9% YoY)。在 ASP 改善、22nm 及特殊製程產
品組合優化，以及產能利用率維持 90%以上的帶動下，預期單位晶圓產值提高，且高稼動率有
助於分攤固定製造成本 ，上調毛利率預估 0.3 個百分點至 41.5%(+8.7ppts YoY)。 隨著矽光子、
TFLN、DTC 及 Hybrid Bonding 等技術持續開發，以及新加坡 P4 與台南 P7、P8 擴產計畫逐
步推進，相關研發、人力及管理費用將隨業務規模擴大而增加，故預估營業費用年增 37.9%至
416.00 億元，調升營業利益預估 1.0%至 1,113.59 億元(+79.4% YoY)，稅後淨利 1,038.76 億
元(+13.6% YoY)，EPS 8.35 元，營運再創歷史新高。

### Page 5

成熟製程需求改善及高價值特殊製程逐步放量，聯電稼動率維持高檔 
聯電短期受惠成熟製程供需結構改善，AI 相關電源管理、FPGA 及連接晶片需求持續成長，帶
動 8 吋與 12 吋產能利用率同步提升，3Q26 整體稼動率達 92%，部分製程已接近滿載。展望
27 年，隨產能供給趨緊及高附加價值產品占比提升，營運成長動能將由出貨量增加轉向 ASP
改善與產品組合優化，中長期則持續深化 22nm 特殊製程、矽光子、TFLN、DTC 及先進封裝
布局 ， 並透過新加坡與台南雙軌擴產計畫 ， 加速AI 相關產能建置 ， 其中12 吋 PIC 與 8 吋 TFLN
平台已支援 1.6T 高速光通訊應用，預計 27 年逐步增加營收貢獻，隨 28 年底後新增產能陸續
開出，進一步推升AI 相關業務占比，帶動公司由傳統成熟製程代工轉向高附加價值特殊製程及
異質整合平台。 
目前聯電股價交易在 128 元，位於近三年參考本益比區間 9~22x 平均值位置，考量成熟製程
復甦加快，稼動率攀升，且先進封裝與矽光子將開始貢獻營收，維持買進投資建議，因上調 27
年獲利預估，本益比由平均值之 16 倍調高至中間偏上之 18 倍，目標價由 130 元(16X 修正前
27(F) PER)調升至 150 元(18X 修正後 27(F) PER)。

### Page 6

公司簡介 
聯電是一家專注於純晶圓代工的半導體公司 ， 其核心競爭力並不在於先進製程節點的技術領先 ，
而是在成熟製程的量產能力、良率穩定度、成本控制與長期客戶關係。公司長期深耕 28 奈米
以上製程，並在顯示驅動 IC、影像感測、嵌入式非揮發記憶體、高壓與功率元件等特色製程平
台建立規模優勢。從產業定位來看，聯電更接近景氣循環型但具結構性支撐的公司，而非高度
技術競賽導向的成長型公司。

在獲利結構上，聯電的關鍵驅動因子主要來自稼動率與產品組合的變化。成熟製程的固定成本
比重高，因此當整體需求回溫、產能利用率上升時，毛利率通常會呈現明顯改善。相反地，若
需求疲弱導致稼動率下滑，即使營收跌幅有限，獲利仍可能受到放大衝擊。此外，營收成長的
品質也高度依賴產品組合，來自特色製程或車用、工控平台的訂單，對平均售價與毛利率的貢
獻，明顯優於一般消費性成熟製程產品。

成熟製程市場本身具有較強的商品化特性，價格與供需循環對聯電的短中期表現影響甚大。相
較先進製程由少數廠商主導，成熟製程更容易受到同業擴產、價格競爭以及客戶庫存調整的影
響。因此，在研究聯電時，不能僅以公司自身營運為出發點，而必須同時評估整體成熟製程產
能是否出現結構性過剩，以及終端市場是否進入補庫存或去庫存階段。

圖 2. Revenue by Application

資料來源: UMC、國泰證期研究部整理

在中長期結構上，車用與工業應用被視為聯電相對穩定的成長支柱。這類應用具備產品生命週
期長 、 轉換成本高與品質要求嚴格等特性 ， 有助於降低價格波動與需求急劇反轉的風險 。 然而，
其導入與認證週期較長，對短期財務數字的拉動效果有限，因此更適合作為評估聯電估值下緣
與營運穩定度的依據，而非短線成長動能。

### Page 7

聯電 ESG 政策 
聯華電子股份有限公司以創造以人為本、與環境共生、與社會共榮的全球性友善生態新價值作
為永續發展的核心願景，將環境(Environmental)、社會(Social)與公司治理(Governance)全面
整合至公司營運策略與決策架構之中 。 聯電認為 ， 企業長期競爭力不僅來自於技術與製造能力，
更奠基於穩健治理、負責任的環境管理，以及對員工、供應鏈與社會的正向影響。基於此，聯
電持續透過制度化的 ESG 政策與行動方案，確保公司在追求營運成長的同時，能有效降低對
環境與社會所造成的衝擊，並回應利害關係人之期待。

1. 公司治理： 
在公司治理面向，聯電以董事會作為  ESG 治理之最高層級，負責督導永續策略方向與重大決
策。董事會下設之永續發展暨提名委員會由全體獨立董事組成，專責監督  ESG 治理成效，而 
ESG 指導委員會則由共同總經理擔任主席，結合多位核心高階主管，負責永續策略目標的訂定
與執行。透過此一由上而下的治理體系，聯電確保永續議題能夠實質納入公司策略、資本配置
與績效管理之中，並定期向董事會報告執行成果，使  ESG 不僅停留於政策宣示，而是成為公
司治理的一部分。 
圖 3. ESG 治理組織架構圖

資料來源:UMC、國泰證期研究部整理

2. 風險治理與誠信經營： 
在風險治理與誠信經營方面，聯電將  ESG 議題全面納入企業風險管理架構，依循  COSO 企
業風險管理原則，系統性辨識、評估並管理營運過程中可能產生的治理、環境與社會風險。企
業風險管理委員會負責跨部門整合風險資訊，並與  ESG 指導委員會保持密切連結，確保氣候
變遷、供應鏈、人權、資訊安全等重大永續風險，能即時反映於公司決策與因應策略之中。此
一治理設計，使聯電能在高度變動的產業環境中，維持營運穩健與長期韌性。 
圖 4. 企業風險管理組織架構圖

資料來源:UMC、國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 8

3. 環境政策： 
在環境政策方面，聯電承諾以低碳轉型與資源效率為核心，朝 2050 年淨零排放目標邁進，並
積極響應 RE100 再生能源倡議。公司持續推動氣候行動，透過製程改善、設備升級與能源結
構轉型，有效降低溫室氣體排放。至 2024 年，聯電範疇一與範疇二溫室氣體排放量已較基準
年減量 31%， 顯示公司正穩健地行走於既定的減碳路徑上 。 同時 ， 聯電亦導入內部碳定價機制，
將每噸二氧化碳當量的成本納入投資與管理決策，藉此驅動更多具實質效益的減碳行動。

圖 5. 環境目標與績效表

資料來源:UMC、國泰證期研究部整理

4. 水資源管理： 
除氣候行動外，聯電亦重視水資源管理、廢棄物減量與生物多樣性保護。公司透過提升再生水
與海淡水使用比例，降低對天然水資源的依賴，同時推動廢棄物資源化與循環經濟模式，持續
減少單位產品的環境足跡。在污染防制方面，聯電針對空氣污染與水污染設有嚴格管理機制，
確保排放表現優於法規標準，並逐步將自然相關風險與機會納入長期環境管理規劃。

5. 社會政策 
在社會政策方面，聯電以人權保障與人才發展為核心，致力打造安全、健康、多元與共融的職
場環境。公司遵循國際人權準則與責任商業聯盟行為守則，確保員工在招聘、任用、升遷與薪
酬制度中受到公平對待，並透過完善的職業安全與健康管理體系，持續降低職災風險。在人才
培育與留任方面，聯電透過教育訓練、職涯發展與員工持股等制度，強化員工向心力與長期承
諾，使人力資本成為支持公司永續成長的重要基礎。

投資評等定義

BUY 買進 介於+15% ~ +30%

台灣央行利率連十凍，川習會前瞻，
高利環境與期中選舉下的新亞操作
分析師／吳雨桑
研究範疇／新興市場 / 新興亞洲 / 中國 / 香港/ 台灣
新興亞洲投資攻略
出 刊 日 : 2026/9/24
熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
市場投資展望
資產 4Q26看法 投資展望
中國兩年期殖利率 1.25% 景氣下行壓力持續，本行仍預期接下來有降準50bp空間。
新興市場股 中立 油價高檔拉鋸，主要國家央行貨幣政策偏向緊縮，債務堆壘隱憂揮之不去，高利環境下，兼具基本面穩健與政策操作有空間的新亞、拉美相對正向。
新興亞洲 中立 新興亞洲中相對看好台灣、韓國、東協與印度，AI推論的供不應求，適時留意評價面空間循序參與台韓投資契機，印度有評價面保護優勢。

中國A 中立 中國景氣K型化加劇，投資方向維持選股不選市，聚焦受惠AI相關供應鏈兼國產替代政策扶植的科技族群、稀缺原物料及高附加出口價值等產業。
香港 中立 削價競爭雖告尾聲但內需乏力影響獲利，並關注資本支出轉為獲利進程。
台灣 正向 長線台股AI供應鏈握技術進程優勢與漲價潮，擇評價面分批布局台股。
• 修正後資金重拾AI投資議題 P1

1
《投資關鍵-修正過後資金重拾AI投資議題》
9月全球股市歷經主要國家貨幣政策轉趨鷹派，市場
對政府債務堆壘的疑慮 ，主要國家公債殖利高檔拉
鋸影響對風險性資產的偏好 。惟歷經適度修正後 ，
市場重拾對AI投資主題的信心 ，台韓股市肩負重要
AI基礎建設的供應鏈要角 ，評價調整後重新吸引資
金進駐，在全球股市震盪之際中表現相對亮眼。

《投資關鍵-9月板塊輪動，AI題材重獲青睞》
9月中國股市呈現板塊輪動格局，能源與原物料價格
走勢波動，資金從先前漲幅較大的原材料與能源出
走，消費板塊因內需不振今年以來持續疲軟 。資金

族群，顯見AI算力鏈與國產替代加持下表現穩健。
